# Chat UI review, 2026-10-21

This report covers the Virtues chat UI: the new-chat screen, the composer and its turn controls, what shows while the assistant works (the ∴ mark, the status line, the steps fold), the reply and everything the assistant draws inside it, approvals, citations, long chats, phones and the Mac app. The method had four parts. Nine subsystem audits read the code, and a second adversarial pass re-checked every finding, confirming, correcting or refuting it. Six web research sweeps covered frontier chat apps, power-user agent tools, generative UI and citations, personal-data assistants, motion, and accessibility. Ten perspectives then argued over the results: one per persona, an advocate for the current design, a devil's advocate, a round of creative ideation, and three high-fidelity specs. The report describes the code as of commit 4ceea332c plus uncommitted style edits in the shared checkout, chiefly another agent's Asgard edit in `themes.css`. Eleven commits landed on `wave` during the review (ca63b471e through dee0fe1d8). Most of them move chat, pages and the composer onto one prose stylesheet. The type and stylesheet findings were re-checked against those commits, and the report says so wherever a later commit changed a finding. Nothing in the repo was changed.

**How to read it.** "Confirmed" means a second reading of the code agreed. "Inferred" means it follows from the code but was not run. Line numbers come from the audits' reads and can drift by a few lines. Where an audit recorded both a committed and a working-tree number, the committed one is used and the other is marked "(working tree)". Every example is invented. Personas are abbreviated as NS (narrative seeker), QS (quantified self), Pro (tech prosumer), Rec (recovery) and DS (data sovereign). Owner questions are numbered Q1 to Q42 in section 13.

---

## 1. Summary

**What is strong.**
- The `show` contract: the model cannot type a number into a figure. The box runs read-only SQL and draws one of six fixed kinds (`virtues-core/src/tools/show.rs:7-11`).
- A status line built from the model's own narration and the call in flight, with no random verbs (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:122-143`).
- Rejoin and retry that never start a second billed turn (`ChatView.svelte:477-610`).
- A context view generated by the same code that builds the send (`chat.rs:1078-1117`).
- Quiet web citations with no favicon fetch (`LinkChip.svelte:10-12`).
- A new-chat sentence written by the model that is allowed to say nothing (`chat_opening.rs:209-215`).

**What breaks today.** These are the most serious confirmed problems.
1. A chat started on the local model reopens as a cloud chat, and its next turn sends the saved local history to a provider (composer-4, start-3).
2. Pressing Shift+Tab twice in the composer turns on Sudo, which grants a passwordless shell, with no announcement (composer-5, a11y-1).
3. Math renders as raw TeX (transcript-1).
4. The figure-to-rows citation that the prompt teaches renders as an "@" chat link that opens New Chat (reply-ui-1).
5. Plots from code are drawn twice (working-2).
6. Charts over hours label every point with the same date (reply-ui-3).
7. On an approval card, Deny does nothing, and Allow stays live on old turns (reply-ui-4).
8. Text the composer is told to put back, such as a queued line after Stop or a failed send, never reaches the editor. Pressing Enter while the capability banner is showing erases the message (composer-1, composer-2).
9. The Stop glyph and other icons are fetched from Iconify's public API. The Tauri CSP blocks that request, so they draw blank in the apps (composer-3).
10. The new-chat opening and the generated chat titles ignore the owner's "Never bring up" rules (recovery findings in 10.11).
11. The transcript has no semantics for screen readers (a11y-3).
12. On the phone, opening anything from a reply navigates the only window away from the chat, with no way back (mobile-1).

**Main decisions in this report.**
- Keep the current vocabulary: the ∴ mark, the narrated status line, the folded "Worked for" receipt, figures drawn only from rows, quiet citations, and one model-written sentence on an empty chat. Fix the bugs inside each.
- Cut the brief's widget sketch, because it rebuilds the removed Home page. The new chat grows from the existing sentence (10.5).
- Draw Model, Depth and Access as one row of three controls over today's four server shapes. Combinations the server cannot honor are made unreachable. Full access needs a confirm and is never in a keyboard cycle (10.4).
- Drive the ∴ mark's depth from real passes and running calls instead of from the clock (10.1).
- Use one stylesheet tuned only by tokens. Chat and pages already share it, so the remaining work is token fixes and a reply variant (10.6).
- Two reply primitives. A framed ReplyCard is for something you act on. An unframed ReplyRecord line is for something that happened (10.7).
- Show where work ran inside the steps fold by default. A line under each reply is opt-in (10.11).
- Freeze the figure catalog. Spend the effort on correctness, units, gaps and accessibility (10.10).

**Order of work.** Section 12 has the detail.

| Slice | Content |
|---|---|
| 0 | Trust bugs |
| 1 | Tokens, type, contrast and the motion foundation |
| 2 | The working state |
| 3 | Composer and controls |
| 4 | Reply primitives, approvals and citations |
| 5 | Figures |
| 6 | Provenance and memory |
| 7 | New chat |
| 8 | Long chats |
| 9 | Mobile |
| 10 | Later |

## 2. Where the brief is wrong

### 2.1 Factual errors

| The brief says | What the code does | Evidence |
|---|---|---|
| Intro: the ∴ settles, then "Virtues" rises | The default is today's events drawn as a sky plus one sentence the model writes. The ∴ and the word appear only on a day with no events | `NewChatSky.svelte:86-117` |
| Steps: Open | Built: "Worked for 12s · nouns", which opens on click and has a live region | `ThinkingBlock.svelte:300-421` |
| Charts: Open | Built: bar and line charts with 1-3 series and a readout, plus five other show kinds | `ShowChart.svelte`; `show.rs:36-43` |
| Browsing shows "Browsing example.com", and the Mac app takes over a window | The status line shows step verbs ("Clicking", "Typing", "Waiting for you: …"). The Mac browser is a split pane that the assistant opens on its own | `toolPresentation.ts:172-190`; `browserPaneListener.ts:43` |
| Math: rendered in replies | Not rendered. Streamdown's fallback shows TeX in a code span | `Markdown.svelte:130-145` |
| Chat and pages have separate stylesheets | Shared `prose.css` since ca63b471e. Code blocks, callouts and to-dos were unified in 7a614a6ab and the composer in 0f65dbd6e. What still differs: math, the user's echoed message, Show tables, and card rhythm | `prose.css:17-22`; `Markdown.svelte:134` |
| A context meter appears in the composer at 50% | `ChatContextIndicator` is dead code. Usage is a "Context N%" row in the ⋮ menu, shown at any percentage | `chat/index.ts:10`; `ChatView.svelte:1287-1302` |
| Dictation is an attachment | Dictation exists only in the Mac app, as macOS system dictation, a way of typing | `ChatInput.svelte:252-259` |
| Approvals cover publishing and editing a page | No publish gate exists. `edit_page` runs without asking. Only sudo deletes pause the turn | `executor.rs:288-412`; `sudo_gate.rs:59-75` |
| Legend: 1 dot = Done | The single dot exists only for about 540ms of a 2000ms landing. Then the mark unmounts | `ThinkingMark.svelte:4, 252`; `ThinkingBlock.svelte:313` |
| Legend: 5 dots = Many passes | Five dots means more than 15s elapsed or six or more calls. Passes are not counted | `ThinkingBlock.svelte:79-83, 177-178` |
| The status line has four states | It has six, including "Waiting on your server" (a 45s stall) and "Sitting with this one" (15s) | `ThinkingBlock.svelte:122-143` |
| Composer placeholder "Message" | "Ask Virtues" | `ChatView.svelte:2530` |
| The sketch's "Tuesday, October 21" | October 21 is a Wednesday in 2026. The times are also hardcoded 24-hour, while the app formats times by locale | `SentMessageCard.svelte:142` |

### 2.2 What the assistant knows each turn, as built

The brief lists six prompt parts. The real prompt is nine blocks plus the compaction summary (`chat.rs:1295-1469`). Each user message also opens with a "[Sent Friday, October 9, 2026 at 6:52 PM CDT]" stamp (`compaction.rs:777-792`).

| Order | Block | What it holds | Brief's mistake |
|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | base (cached) | identity line; character and style notes; output format; `<about_their_life>`; the narrative identity when written; tool usage with `<while_you_work>` and citations; page tools when `edit_page` is callable; mode block | The narrative identity is fused into this block, so it cannot be listed separately (`chat.rs:1309-1313`) |
| 2 | precedence | Static text ranking rules, narrative identity and memory | Missing from the brief |
| 3 | memory | Notes in facts, manner and practices lanes | — |
| 4 | circumstances | A clock line, "As of {time} (to the quarter hour; read again hourly)"; identity; chapters; place; today so far; calendar for today and tomorrow; task counts; people in touch over the last 14 days; live threads; last night's sleep; recent days; connected sources (`circumstances.rs:125-509`) | Not "computed fresh": kept per chat for up to an hour, with the clock floored to 15 minutes. "People around you" means recency of contact. Sleep covers last night only. Tasks are counts only |
| 5 | coverage | One date-range line per table | Missing from the brief |
| 6 | active project, scoped line, compaction summary | — | Summary missing from the brief |
| — | cache breakpoint | — | — |
| 7 | skill | None ship today (`skills.rs:55`) | Missing from the brief |
| 8 | active context | The open page | — |
| 9 | rules | The owner's rules, placed last and never cached | — |

The backend audit's summary line "the current time is not in the prompt" is wrong. The circumstances block carries the clock line. Only the exact send time rides on the user message.

### 2.3 Contradictions inside the brief

1. It asks for "calm by default" and, in the same document, for widgets, a context meter, three controls, a thinking legend, a status line, and moments worth sharing. Section 7.3 adds up what they would cost on screen.
2. It says anything above the composer slows a quick question, then sketches a dateline, a greeting and three cards above the composer.
3. The sketch's "Good morning." and its "7h 12m avg" both break the opening's own prompt rules: "Do not greet them … or mention counts, totals, distances or durations" (`chat_opening.rs:213`).
4. The sketch matches the removed Home page: "A dateline, a novelty line, the day's streams as a deck" (`agents/build/design-grammar.md:390-392`).
5. "1 dot = Done" conflicts with "settle back to the mark", because the resting mark has three dots.
6. "Local" appears as a Model value beside Depth and Access, yet Local has no tools and no prompt from the chat builder (`chat_mode.rs:80-83`).
7. "Depth: Deep research" makes the mode picker permanent. The owner's recorded direction is that Deep research becomes a skill (Q33).
8. "Prefer micro-craft" conflicts with a widget homepage.
9. The out-of-scope list conflicts with the asks. A notifications widget needs the notifications plan, which still routes "Needs you" to the removed Home (`notifications-plan.md:146, 220, 243-244`).

### 2.4 Persona order

The brief ranks the quantified self as co-primary and the prosumer as secondary. The owner's persona note ranks the narrative seeker first, the quantified self second, and the prosumer, recovery and data sovereign third. This report follows the brief and flags the difference as Q28, because "moments worth sharing" serves the prosumer.

## 3. What each persona needs from chat

| Persona | Asks (invented) | What breaks today | Main fixes |
|---|---|---|---|
| NS | "What was I like the summer I moved?" "How did the trip with David go?" | The opening's line about today is stored at the top of a chat about 2025 (start-4). Record citations open a card that says only "Record" (`RefCard.svelte:32-44`). Quoted words are drawn in muted ink (`prose.css:297-302`). Long messages fold at 8 lines. Copy glues narration onto the reply. No day breaks. Compaction uses a template written for code handoffs (start-7) | Opening threaded only when chosen (10.5); source card with the passage (10.9); quote style with a source line (10.9); fold rule (10.9); day dividers and send-to-top (10.13); life-shaped compaction (10.13); Save as a page (10.9) |
| QS | "How have I been sleeping since the new job?" "What did I spend eating out this month vs last?" | Same-date axis labels; months read as "Oct 1"; missing weeks are bridged; zero looks the same as missing; no units (money arrives in cents); the source line counts aggregate rows; the figure citation is broken; plots drawn twice | Units, buckets, gaps, an open-period mark, a count and window in the source line, a "usual" band later (10.10) |
| Pro | Wants polish and things worth showing | Blank Stop icon, raw TeX, a send icon that rotates with overshoot on every Enter, five working signals at different rhythms, Title Case copy | Basics first (slice 0), then the moments in section 11 |
| Rec | "How am I doing with the early walks?" | The opening and titles can surface a setback the owner asked never to be raised; memory writes are invisible; capture gaps read as lapses | `avoid` rules applied to the opening and titles, a memory receipt, gaps drawn as gaps, a contract against streaks and causal verbs (10.11) |
| DS | "Did that leave my server?" | A local chat reopens as cloud; opening a new chat sends today's events to a cloud model with nothing on screen; the context panel's Provider row is always "—"; nothing says what left the box | Local stays local (B11), "Where this ran" in the fold, a provider row, an export of the calls that left the box (10.11) |

## 4. What works and should stay

- **The ∴ mark as a claim about depth.** It uses the app icon's geometry, runs no animation loop under reduced motion, and its `depth` prop is documented as "a claim, not a mood" (`ThinkingMark.svelte:15-48, 334-366`).
- **The status line's priority order.** It uses the model's narration, then the call described from its arguments, then plain fallbacks (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:105-143`; `prompt.rs:114-121`).
- **"Worked for".** This is more accurate than other apps' "Thought for", because the span includes tools and writing (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:322-343`).
- **One presentation table for tools, with a drift test** (`toolPresentation.ts:1-15`).
- **TurnPhaseController.** It gives one answer to "is a turn working", with a 45s stall and a 300s let-go (`turnPhase.svelte.ts`).
- **The show contract.** Refusals name the fix, times are read on the owner's clock, bars start at zero, and the readout line replaces tooltips (`show.rs`; `show.ts:91-106`; `ShowChart.svelte:57-62, 152-165`).
- **Rejoin.** A 1500ms floor, and Try again rejoins first, then re-reads, then regenerates (`ChatView.svelte:477-610`).
- **StoppedNotice's eight reasons**, each naming who ended the reply (`StoppedNotice.svelte:4-56`).
- **Hand-built WebKit scroll anchoring.** `holdReadingPosition` and `composerReserve` both work (`holdReadingPosition.ts:31-74`; `composerReserve.ts:13-45`).
- **The composer's editor kernel.** Its key map is written down and tested case by case (`composer.ts:10-18`; `composer.corpus.ts`; `composer.test.ts`).
- **Quiet web citations** with no favicon request (`LinkChip.svelte:10-12`).
- **The rail** is built from the message array and uses a binary-search scroll spy (`Rail.svelte:88-139`; `transcript.ts:334-356`).
- **The context view** is generated by the send's own code (`chat.rs:1078-1117`).
- **Owner rules** come in two kinds and are placed last on every turn (`chat.rs:930-977`). Notes the owner edits cannot be rewritten by the model (`assistant_memories.rs:55-70`).
- **Maps** use tiles from the box, so no tile provider learns where the owner has been (`ShowMap.svelte:2-4`).
- **The ThreeBody easter egg** (`ThreeBody.svelte`, `threeBody.ts`).

### 5.2 Corrections to the audits

The verify passes corrected several claims. Do not build on the uncorrected versions.

- **type-5 (refuted).** The composer already reads `prose prose--compact` (0f65dbd6e). It is not a separate copy.
- **transcript-15.** Code blocks and callouts are already unified (7a614a6ab). Only math, the user echo, Show tables and card rhythm remain.
- **a11y-9.**
  - Committed Asgard measures 3.16:1. The 3.36 figure comes from another agent's uncommitted edit.
  - Four light themes also fail subtle ink on `--surface-elevated`: Caladan, Rivendell and Netherfield at 4.35, Hogwarts at 4.16.
  - Three suggested replacement values still fail: Lyceum #9AA2AE at 4.17, Tatooine #6E7069 at 4.10, Agora #8A97C4 at 4.10.
- **type-11, reply-ui-5.** New boxes default to Oxford (`theme.ts:58-61`). Pemberley is only the `:root` fallback. Its flat white is a stated intent (`themes.css:249-256`), so do not argue from Pemberley as the default. Q38 covers applying a ground there.
- **start-23.** The LocalModelCard title is an `<h2>`, so it takes the global serif and is set at weight 600. That is faux bold and breaks the never-bold rule. It is not a sans title.
- **working-16.** BrowserView already shows a `role=status` handoff bar with "I can't" and "Done" (`BrowserView.svelte:307-313`). A chat card must not duplicate those controls.
- **mobile-11.** Chat already shows "Waiting for you: <reason>" during a handoff (`toolPresentation.ts:187`).
- **transcript-24.** The claim that a network failure marks the turn dangling is refuted. Only the rejoin clock bug stands.
- **knows-4, knows-10.**
  - `start-step` and `finish-step` already reach the client (`turn_recorder.rs:312-314`).
  - Model and provider arrive on reload (`chats.rs:181-189`).
  - Local stats arrive as an event.
  - What is missing is usage, cost, route, retention and step index during the stream.
- **reply-ui-11.** `edit_page`'s `needs_binding` is at `page_editor.rs:588` and carries no `page_id`, so the card has no button at all.
- **reply-ui-19.** Temporary chats already tell the model that images will not show (`tools/executor.rs:697-703`, working tree).
- **working-12.** Only a start time is stored per call. Per-call durations need B3.
- **working-1.** `holdReadingPosition` corrects most of the collapse. The 38px jump shows only when the anchor sits above the block.
- **composer-5.** The mode chip does show the name and description. Only the screen-reader announcement is missing.
- **composer-7.** WebKit does not focus a button on click.
- **composer-8.** Tab already reaches Stop.
- **composer-20.** The empty-state glide does have a reduced-motion override.
- **Reduced-motion helpers.** One helper already exists at `apps/web/src/lib/components/setup/motion.ts:15` (`reducedMotion()`), with a private copy at `document/drag-handle.ts:138`. Section 10.6 picks one approach.

---

## 6. What other products do

**Where Virtues is ahead.**
- The status line from narration. Claude.ai and ChatGPT show a timer and a summary.
- "Worked for" in place of "Thought for".
- Figures that cite rows the box ran. In 2026 ChatGPT's Intelligent UI, Claude's inline visuals and Gemini's dynamic view all moved toward model-generated UI, which takes longer and sometimes contains errors.

**The largest gaps against the frontier**, ranked by value to the personas:
1. Editing and resending a sent question.
2. Navigation for long chats.
3. A per-turn statement of what ran on the box and what left it.
4. A record of what memory or which records a reply used.
5. A visible queued follow-up.

**Patterns adopted, and where they land.**

| Pattern | Source | Section |
|---|---|---|
| Move focus or scroll to anchor the question near the top with a peek of the previous turn | shadcn message scroller; ChatGPT web (from memory) | 10.13 |
| A memory receipt with change and forget at the point of writing | Le Chat "clickable receipts"; ChatGPT "Memory updated"; Oura memories | 10.11 |
| A per-request outbound log the owner can export | Apple Intelligence Report | 10.11 |
| Typical-range band; two-of-several agreement for outliers in the prompt | Apple Vitals; Oura | 10.10 |
| Partial current period marked, both windows named | Dr. Drang on Apple Health Trends | 10.10 |
| Strength and count behind a correlation; the cause left to the owner | Exist.io | 10.11 |
| No reset-to-zero counters; credit effort; allow backfill; say when capture stopped | Jones, Yuan, Yarosh (IMWUT 2021); I Am Sober forum | 10.11 |
| A quote with context shown only when the match is confident | Glean deep-linked citations | 10.9 |
| Literal command on the approval card; keyboard answers; never "always" for deletes | Raycast, Zed | 10.8 |
| Saved prompts on "/" | Granola Recipes, Linear skills | 10.3 |
| Queue, steer at the next tool boundary, send now | Cursor, Zed | 10.3, 11 |
| One persistent empty live region for start and finish | azukiazusa streaming test; TetraLogical | 10.2 |
| An article per turn with an sr-only sender heading | Claude Code a11y issues #86874, #58428 | 10.13 |
| Chart summary label, data table, arrow-key points | Highcharts, Chartability, Olli, Apple HIG | 10.10 |
| Frequency decides motion; ease-out for entrances; no overshoot on chrome | Emil Kowalski, Rauno Freiberg, Apple HIG | 10.6, Appendix A |

**Not adopted:** the anti-patterns listed in 7.2.

**Limits of the research.**
- The shared web-search budget ran out before Grok's thinking display, Le Chat's 2026 UI, Meta AI's chat and GPT-6's reasoning display could be checked.
- `help.openai.com` returned 403, so every OpenAI item is secondhand.
- Three details are from memory and should be checked on a device before anyone designs around them: ChatGPT anchoring the sent question at the top, Claude's "‹ 1/2 ›" arrows, and Apple's "Confirm ChatGPT Requests".

---

## 7. Proposals weighed

### 7.1 The popular proposals, and what this report does with them

| Proposal | Strongest case against | Strongest case for | Resolution |
|---|---|---|---|
| Widgets on a new chat | They rebuild the removed Home; fixed tiles replace model judgment; no endpoint combines today's data; push sends nothing; no room on a phone with the keyboard up; more of the record goes to a cloud model on every New chat | One glance at the day; screenshots for the prosumer | **Cut as sketched.** The sky becomes readable, the sentence becomes a button, up to two more model-chosen lines (Q10), and a "Needs you" line from turn state (Q32). See 10.5 |
| Three controls: Model, Depth, Access | The axes are coupled in `ChatMode` (`chat_mode.rs:63-84`); "Quick + Full access" would cap a server change at 8 steps; Local ignores Depth and Access; a permanent row adds chrome for the NS | Discoverable; a keyboard user can see and change the turn's shape | **One row over today's four shapes.** Impossible combinations are unreachable (Deep research shows Access as "Reads only"; a small-model chat locks the row). Quick waits on B18. Q33 asks whether Deep research becomes a skill instead. See 10.4 |
| A 1-5 dot legend | Today the dots measure time; motion is the only carrier; the 3.2px dots are hard to read | A mark from the app's own geometry, worth sharing | **Keep the mark, drive depth from real signals** (10.1). No legend appears in the UI. The brief's legend is rewritten |
| One shared stylesheet | Already done; forcing the user echo and data tables into it brings back the VIR-333 misrender and restyles every page table | — | **Token API plus a reply variant.** User echo is Q41; open tables on pages is Q14. See 10.6 |
| Generative UI everywhere | The six kinds carry most of the high-severity bugs; each new kind repeats validation, themes, accessibility and phone work | Charts in replies are the QS persona's main feature | **Freeze the catalog at six kinds.** `range` and `point` later (10.10). Model-written HTML ruled out |
| Moments worth sharing | Optimizes for the lowest-ranked persona's audience; puts a life record up for sharing; the most frequent surfaces get the most motion | Polish matters to the prosumer | **Only hidden craft and features with nothing personal in them by default.** Share-as-image waits on Q9. See 11 |
| A provenance line under every reply | The data was not on the client; a short label cannot be both short and true; cost under every reply teaches metering | The DS asked for it | **"Where this ran" in the fold by default.** Line under each reply is opt-in, with two exceptions that always show. See 10.11 |
| Circumstances chips above the composer | Stale location (knows-13); the clock is up to an hour old; names contacts on a living-room screen | Shows what the assistant knows | **Cut.** Scope appears only where a real scope exists (a mention, a bound page, a project) |
| Questions that wait for data | No push; unattended turns are cancelled after 300s (`live_turn.rs:31`); a trigger on missing data reads as an accusation for the Rec persona | Proactive help | **Cut from chat.** Revisit as task requests once they prove themselves |
| A context meter in the composer | A percentage on the surface every persona watches | Warns before compaction at 85% | **A 14px ring in the controls row from 50%, pending Q30.** The menu row stays either way |

### 7.2 Not building

- Widgets as sketched.
- A dateline or greeting on new chats.
- A proactive feed.
- Check-ins that rely on push.
- Questions that wait for data.
- Circumstances chips.
- Model-written HTML in replies.
- Streaks, reset-to-zero counters, badges, scores or colored bands for health or recovery data.
- Success haptics or confetti.
- A separate health or recovery mode. Skills and rules cover it.
- Thumbs up and down. Nobody reads them on a box with one owner.
- A percentage for how much of a reply came from the records.
- Numbers that count up.
- Blur-in animations under reduced motion.
- An Apple Intelligence-style rainbow glow.
- Random status verbs.
- Generic source footers.
- Numbered citation badges.
- An empty slash menu.
- DOM windowing that breaks the rail. Use `content-visibility` instead.
- Promising phone alerts while APNs sends nothing.

### 7.3 What the composer would cost on a phone with the keyboard up

These figures are computed from the specs in this report. Device and keyboard heights are inferred: iPhone SE 375×667 with a keyboard of about 260pt, and iPhone 15 393×852 with about 336pt. Visible pane = height − top safe area − 48px bar − keyboard. That gives 339px on the SE and 409px on the iPhone 15.

| Stack | Height | Transcript left (SE / 15) |
|---|---|---|
| One-line pill (48) + row margin (6) + controls row (24) + padding (8) | 86 | 253 / 323 |
| + reply-to chip (24 + 8 gap) | 118 | 221 / 291 |
| + one queued chip (30 + 8) | 156 | 183 / 253 |
| + attachments row inside the pill (56 + 6) | 218 | 121 / 191 |
| + a second line of draft (24) | 242 | 97 / 167 |

On the SE, every extra row costs about a tenth of the readable transcript. That is why nothing above the composer is permanent, why the phone row is one tap target, and why section 10.12 caps the editor at 30% of visible height.

## 10. Specs

### 10.1 The ∴ thinking mark

**What the dots mean.** The single dot appears only during the landing, for under a second. The resting form of the mark is the three-dot ∴. A finished turn shows no mark, except a turn waiting on the owner. Write this legend into the brief. The UI does not show it.

| What you see | What it means |
|---|---|
| ∴ moving, 3 dots | Thinking, or writing with what it already has |
| 4 dots | A call is running now: your records, the web, a file, your browser |
| 5 dots | Many passes: step 3 or later, 3 or more calls at once, or research workers |
| A single point, under a second | The answer landed. The point opens into ∴, then the mark leaves |
| ∴ still, one step lighter | Your turn. The assistant is waiting for you |

**States.** Each state is driven by an event the box already sends, or one listed in 12.1.

| State | Entered when | Mark | Label | Shimmer |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Sending | `turnPhase.beginSend()` | depth 3 | "Thinking" | on |
| Summarizing | `data-phase {phase:"compacting"}` before `start` (B4) | 3 | "Summarizing earlier messages" | on |
| Reasoning | `reasoning-start`, or `start-step` with nothing running | 3; floor 4 in Deep research | "Thinking" | on |
| Calling | `tool-input-start` | 4, or 5 per the rule below | narration clause, else the call | on |
| Fan-out | `dispatch_subagents` running | 5 | "Searching 3 ways at once · 1 of 3 done" | on |
| Browsing | `data-subagent` titled `browse` with a step | 4 | "Clicking · example.com" (B7) | on |
| Writing | committed reply text at or after `bodyFromIndex`, no call running | 3 | "Writing" | on |
| Stalled | 45s with no chunk and no call running | sustained form of the current depth | "Waiting on your server" | off |
| Your turn, live | running `browser_handoff`, or a browse step with op `handoff` | still REST, subtle ink | "Waiting for you in your browser" | off |
| Your turn, paused | finish `other` and the last tool output has `awaiting_owner`, `needs_binding` or `permission_needed` | still REST, subtle ink | "Waiting for you to allow this" | off |
| Landing | `finish` reason `stop`, no stop reason, no `chat.error`, not paused | point, then ∴, then leaves | "Worked for 12s · …" | off |
| Stopped or failed | owner's Stop, `abort`, or `chat.error` | frozen pose fades over 150ms | "Worked for 12s · …" | off |
| Finished | after landing or stop | none | "Worked for 12s · your records, the web" | off |

The shimmer means bytes are arriving, so it is off whenever nothing is being produced. Motion means the turn is alive. A still mark in `--color-foreground-subtle` means the turn waits on the owner, using the same pose and ink as RestingMark (`RestingMark.svelte:70-71`).

**Depth rule.** This replaces `ThinkingBlock.svelte:175-187`. The wire already carries `step-start` parts (`turn_recorder.rs:312-314`).

```
stepIndex = count of step-start parts in the turn
running   = tool parts with toolStatus(...) === "running" (show excluded)
mode      = the mode this turn was SENT with (B1 metadata), never the composer's live value

if yourTurn                                     -> still REST (no depth claim)
if writing                                      -> 3
if running includes dispatch_subagents          -> 5
if stepIndex >= 3 || running.length >= 3        -> 5
if running.length >= 1 || lastCallEndedAt < 400ms ago -> 4
else                                            -> mode === "deep_research" ? 4 : 3
```

- An increase in depth takes effect at once.
- A decrease waits until the current depth has been on screen for 600ms, so a 300ms lookup still shows as 4 and the mark does not flicker between calls.
- `sustained` stays at more than 4000ms of elapsed time (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:81`). Elapsed time no longer sets depth.
- Local and Sudo have no floor (working-8). Local is one step with no tools, so it stays at 3.

**Scripts.** Keep all five scripts and their durations: syllogism 1.6s, fold 4.0s, fourth 4.4s, precess 4.2s linear, descent 7.6s (`ThinkingMark.svelte:86-130`). Two fixes:
1. **Precess.** Change its `m3` track from `[[0,0],[0.12,1],[1,1]]` to `[[0,1],[1,1]]`. Today the fourth dot retracts at every loop boundary: at p=0.999 it sits at r 3.19, and at p=0 at r 0. With tilt held at 56° and spin linear, the loop then wraps with no visible seam.
2. **Syllogism.** Its loop boundary sits at about 0.34-0.41 opacity, while every 3D script starts at about 1.0. Always blend between syllogism and a 3D movement.

**Changing movement.** This replaces "wait for the loop to come home" (`ThinkingMark.svelte:324-330`).
- If both movements are 3D (fold, fourth or descent) and the outgoing loop's boundary is 800ms or less away, cut at the boundary. Both poses are REST there.
- Otherwise blend at once over 300ms (`--motion-layout`): freeze the outgoing pose, start the incoming script at p=0, and lerp each dot's cx, cy, r and opacity with t eased by `--ease-premium`.
- On mount, start `running` at the movement for the current depth, read once with `untrack`. Today line 296 hard-codes `"syllogism"`.

**Painting.**
- Each frame, sort the dots by z, far first, and reorder the circles with `appendChild` when the order changes. Today `paint()` ignores z (`ThinkingMark.svelte:269-278`).
- An IntersectionObserver cancels the rAF while the svg is out of view and resumes it from the same elapsed time. A landing that starts offscreen jumps to its end state.

**Ink, size and position.**
- Ink is `--color-foreground-muted` while working and `--color-foreground-subtle` on your turn, with a 200ms color transition.
- Size is 16px in the chat header, 14px in the jump button, and 24px in the rooms (Composing, unchanged).
- On desktop the mark hangs in the reading gutter: `position: absolute; left: -24px; top: 2px; width: 16px; height: 16px`. The label's first letter stays flush with the reply's text edge, and nothing slides when the mark leaves. This depends on slice 0 changing `.message-wrapper` from `contain: layout paint` to `contain: layout style` (`ChatView.svelte:3028`), so the gutter is not clipped.
- In containers under 480px wide the mark sits inline at x=0 with the label 24px after it.

**Still poses** (viewBox −3 −3 30 30). Used under reduced motion and on your turn.

| Depth | Source | Dots: cx, cy, r, opacity |
|---|---|---|
| 3 (REST) | logo | (12, 5, 3, 1) · (4.5, 18, 3, 1) · (19.5, 18, 3, 1) |
| 4 | `poseOf(fourth, 0.5)` | (12.37, 7.55, 2.82, .86) · (20.11, 14.32, 3.17, 1) · (4.62, 12.42, 3.22, 1) · (10.87, 20.36, 2.85, .88) |
| 5 | `poseOf(descent, 0.45)` | (11.91, 7.43, 2.92, .94) · (6.89, 10.63, 3.24, 1) · (17.17, 17.98, 3.36, 1) · (7.39, 18.73, 2.83, .87) · (16.75, 13.67, 2.78, .83) |

These coordinates were computed by running `poseOf` from `ThinkingMark.svelte:173-246` unchanged.

**Landing.** The trigger is `finish` with reason `stop` while `land` is true. `land` becomes `!stopReason && !chat.error && !pausedForOwner` (`ChatView.svelte:2009-2010`). Today a turn paused for Allow plays the landing (working-20). Proposed `LAND_MS = 960` (Q1), with `LAND_CONV = [[0,0],[0.375,1],[0.583,1],[1,0]]`.

| t (ms) | Mark | Label |
|---|---|---|
| 0 | Freeze the pose; start converging (smoothstep per segment) | Shimmer stops; "Writing" fades out over 150ms |
| 0–360 | Dots move to the centroid (12, 13.667); radius goes to 4.3 units | — |
| 200–380 | — | "Worked for 12s · your records" fades in over 180ms on `--ease-premium` |
| 360–560 | Holds as one point | — |
| 560–960 | Opens into REST | — |
| 960–1160 | Opacity 1→0 over 200ms (`--motion-hover-out`) | — |
| 1160 | Mark unmounts; the hanging slot takes no width | — |

In containers under 480px the label crossfade waits until 960ms. The announcer says "Reply finished. Worked for 12 seconds" at t=0.

**Every reply keeps its header** (Q3). Every assistant turn outside the two rooms keeps the header line. A turn with nothing to open renders it as a `<div>` with no chevron and no tab stop. An old row with no timing and nothing to open renders nothing. Today a plain reply drops the block (working-1), so the layout depends on whether the model emitted reasoning.

**One instance per turn.** Key the in-flight assistant row by the user message that started the turn. Render it at Send, and hand its ThinkingBlock the parts when the assistant message arrives. Today the pre-reply block and the in-message block are separate (`ChatView.svelte:1995-2012, 2365-2375`).

### 10.2 The status line and the steps fold

**Header layout.**

```
button.block-header (a <div> when there is nothing to open)
  height 28px; padding 4px 12px; border-radius var(--radius-full)
  font 13px/20px Avenir 400, --color-foreground-muted
  [mark slot] [label: min-width 0, one line, ellipsis] [tail: flex none]
  (pointer: coarse): ::after hit area max(100%, 44px) tall, drawn size unchanged
  block margin-bottom 10px
```

- **Hover on a live header.** `padding-left: 36px; margin-left: -36px`, with no transform. The `--hover-bg` ground fades in behind the mark over `--motion-hover-in` and out over `--motion-hover-out`.
- **Finished header.** Keeps the current slide from rest to hover (`translateX(-12px)` → 0, `ThinkingBlock.svelte:454-476`), retimed to `--motion-state` on `--ease-premium` (working-23).
- **Truncation.** One line everywhere. On pointer devices the header carries `title={label}`. The full text is always in the fold.
- **Elapsed tail.** " · 18s" appears after 10s and ticks every second, in `font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums` and `--color-foreground-muted`.
- **Location tail.** Shown by default only in Local chats, as " · on your server". With "Show where each reply ran" on (10.11), every chat shows it: " · on your server" while a box tool runs, " · with {provider display name}" while a model step streams, " · on the web" (web_search), " · in your browser on your Mac" (browse), " · on your Mac" (send_imessage). The tail crossfades by opacity over 150ms and is never announced. It is text only, with no glyph.
- **Shimmer** (Q2). The ink sweeps from `--color-foreground-muted` to `--color-foreground`. `background-size: 400px 100%`, repeated along x, with `background-position-x` animated from 0 to −400px over 3.2s **linear**. That is a constant 125px/s at any label width and twice the mark's 1.6s period. Today's sweep is 200% of the label's width on ease-in-out (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:526-549`), so it speeds up on long labels and lingers at each end. The CSS is in Appendix A.

**Label priority.** This replaces `ThinkingBlock.svelte:122-143`.

1. Your turn: "Waiting for you in your browser" on the Mac, "Waiting for you on your Mac" on the phone, or "Waiting for you to allow this" when paused.
2. Browse step: "{Verb} · {host}", for example "Clicking · example.com", with the host in `--color-foreground` (B7). Before the first step: "Opening example.com".
3. Fan-out: "Searching {n} ways at once · {done} of {n} done".
4. The narration clause for the **current step**, up to 90 characters. Count calls between `step-start` parts. Today `callsSince === 1` drops the clause whenever more than one call follows (`transcript.ts:139`), and the prompt asks for parallel calls (`prompt.rs:108`).
5. Calls running with no clause. One call: `describeTool(part, true, true)`. Two: "Looking in your records and the web". Three or more: "Looking in 3 places at once".
6. "Writing".
7. "Summarizing earlier messages" (B4).
8. "Waiting on your server".
9. "Thinking".

Delete "Sitting with this one" (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:142`). The elapsed tail carries the same information.

**Changing the label.**
- A label derived from a tool must stay true for 250ms before it shows, so an 80ms lookup never flashes. A narration clause shows at once.
- A shown label stays at least 700ms. If newer labels arrive during that hold, keep only the newest. Terminal labels skip the hold: "Writing", either your-turn label, and the finished text.
- When the old and new labels share a prefix ("Searching your messages" → "Searching your calendar"), only the tail changes. The old tail fades out over 120ms. The new tail fades in over 180ms, starting at 60ms, on `--ease-premium`. No translate and no blur. A blur on background-clip text is expensive in WebKit.
- The header height never changes.

**Narration that streams into the reply and then jumps** (working-13, transcript-8). While streaming, a text run after the turn's last call is provisional. It commits to the body once it reaches 160 characters, or after 900ms with no `tool-input-start`. While provisional, the label reads "Writing". If a committed run then becomes narration, it collapses: height to 0 over 300ms and opacity to 0 over 150ms, on `--ease-premium`. It never unmounts in a single frame.

**Special cases.**
- **Stop.** The mark freezes and fades over 150ms, with no landing. The cause appears in one place only, as a ReplyRecord line under the reply (10.7). A stop before any assistant message renders "Stopped before it answered" under the user message. The announcer says "Stopped".
- **Live partial endings.** From B5, the `finish` metadata `{subject}` renders the same line live that a reload renders. Today "length" and "max_steps/budget" endings are silent until reload (transcript-4).
- **Error.** Same as Stop. ChatError (`role=alert`) announces, and the status region says nothing more.
- **Approval pause.** The mark blends to REST over 300ms and its ink moves to subtle over 200ms. It stays still. This is the one finished turn that keeps a mark. When the owner answers, it fades over 200ms.
- **Browser handoff.** The turn is still running. The stall timer and the 300s let-go (`turnPhase.svelte.ts:24`) are suspended for up to the box's 15-minute handoff wait (`browse.rs:31-35`; B9). A ReplyRecord shows the request (10.7). The controls stay in the Browser pane.
- **Research workers.** Delete `SubagentPanel.svelte` and its text "∴" glyph. Workers become rows nested under "Searched 3 ways at once" in the fold (B8). Model ids and token counts move to the context view.
- **Local model.** Depth is fixed at 3. The tail and the finished header both say "on your server".
- **Rejoin.** The clock comes from the server's `startedAt` (B1). Prefer the server span over the client measure (`ChatView.svelte:2006-2008` today uses the shorter one).
- **Jump to latest.** Replace the three-dot wave (`ChatView.svelte:2476`) with a 14px ThinkingMark at the same depth. Show it only while the live header is out of view, which the IntersectionObserver above already knows. Otherwise show the plain arrow. The button stays 36px.
- **Secondary loaders** (tool rows, the code card, figure placeholders): show only after 200ms, then keep visible at least 300ms.

**Finished header copy.**

| Case | Header |
|---|---|
| Measured | "Worked for 12s · your records, the web" |
| Over a minute | "Worked for 1m 4s · …" |
| Under a second | "Worked for 1s" (round up) |
| More than 3 nouns | "your records, the web, a page and 2 more" |
| Local | "Worked for 6s · on your server" |
| Unmeasured, has steps | "Worked on this · your records" |
| Paused | "Waiting for you to allow this", then "Worked for 8s · …" |

Nouns are lowercase. Change "Messages" and "Tasks" to lowercase in `toolPresentation.ts:194, 206, 215` (working-26). Name tables in people's words: `data_calendar_event` → "calendar", `data_communication_message` → "messages", `data_location_point` → "location history". Fall back to "your data" (working-19).

**The steps fold.**
- **Container.** `margin-top: 6px; padding: 12px 16px`, `--hover-bg` ground, `--radius-xl`, 13/20. Remove `max-height: 500px` and its inner scroll (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:574-575`). A long fold shows its first 6 step groups, then a TextAction "Show all 14 steps".
- **Motion.** Opens over 180ms (`--motion-enter`) on `--ease-premium`, with content opacity 0→1 over 120ms starting at 40ms. Closes over 150ms. The chevron rotates over 150ms.
- **Open state** lives in a view-level `Map<messageId, boolean>`.
- **"Show the working"** is a per-viewer setting in the chat ⋮ menu that opens every fold by default. Stored in localStorage `virtues.chat.showWorking`, read and written inside try/catch.
- **Order.** A timeline in part order, grouped by `step-start`. On reload this needs B2.

```
│ The question is about June, so the calendar comes first.            reasoning
Checking your calendar and messages for June.                           narration
●  Read your calendar                        on your server · 0.3s · 9 rows
●  Read your messages                        on your server · 0.6s · 41 rows

●  Searched the web for "tile grout drying time"
                                             sent to the web · 2.1s · 8 results
○  Opened a file · didn't finish                         on your server
●  Wrote the reply                                                    3.4s
```

- **Narration.** `--color-foreground-muted`, rendered through `prose prose--compact` so refs and citations stay links.
- **Reasoning.** Muted, with a 2px `--color-border` rule on the left and 10px padding. Clamped at 4 lines with a "Show all" TextAction. `think` calls render as reasoning.
- **Call row.** `grid-template-columns: 12px minmax(0,1fr) auto; column-gap: 8px; row-gap: 6px; align-items: baseline`. The description uses `describeTool` with full arguments at 13px in `--color-foreground`. The meta is 12px tabular, right-aligned, in `--color-foreground-subtle`. In containers under 420px it wraps under the description, indented 20px.
- **Status glyph.** A 12×12 svg, `aria-hidden`, carrying status only. Location is carried by text.

  | Status | Glyph | Row text |
  |---|---|---|
  | done | filled circle r 2, subtle | — |
  | running | filled circle r 2, foreground | the row text shimmers |
  | failed | filled circle r 2, `--color-error` | " · failed: {summary}" in `--color-error` |
  | didn't finish | ring r 1.75, 1px, subtle | " · didn't finish" |

- **Location suffix** (B6): "on your server", "on your Mac", "sent to the web" (Q24 for the wording).
- **Duration.** From B3. Watched turns can stamp event arrival on the client.
- **Sources** are TextActions styled like `.query-ref`. "9 rows" opens the rows view. "8 results" expands titles with domains as quiet links and fetches no favicons. "14 steps" expands the browse result's `steps` array.
- **Workers** are nested 20px under the dispatch row: mission title, then "done · 12s" or "failed · 4s". A running worker shows its current call on a second muted line (B8).
- **Last row** is "Wrote the reply" with its duration. Below it comes the "Where this ran" footer (10.11).

**Reduced motion.**
- Read `prefersReducedMotion.current` through the shared helper (10.6). It reacts to changes; `ThinkingBlock.svelte:273-274` reads the preference once.
- The mark paints still poses with a 150ms opacity crossfade, and no landing.
- The label has no shimmer. Changes crossfade by opacity over 120ms.
- The fold opens instantly, and running rows read "running" in their meta.
- The header hover is a fill change only.
- ThreeBody's 300ms fade becomes 0 (`ThreeBody.svelte:282`).

**The announcer.** One element in ChatView: `sr-only`, `role="status"`, `aria-live="polite"`, mounted empty when the view mounts and never re-created. To set it, clear it, then write after 100ms. It replaces ThinkingBlock's region (`ThinkingBlock.svelte:297`) and Composing's region, which mounts with its text already inside (`Composing.svelte:44-51`).

| Moment | Text |
|---|---|
| Send | "Working on a reply" (rooms: "Composing a reply") |
| 20s in, if the label changed since the last announcement | "Still working: {label}". After that, at most once every 30s and only on change |
| Stall | "Still waiting on your server" |
| Handoff | "Your browser needs you: {reason}" |
| Approval pause | "Your assistant is waiting for you to allow this" |
| Finish | "Reply finished. Worked for 12 seconds" |
| Stop | "Stopped" |
| Error | nothing (ChatError is `role=alert`) |

The fold is an `<ol aria-label="Steps">`. Each row starts with an sr-only status word: "Done:", "Running:", "Failed:", "Didn't finish:".

### 10.3 The composer

**Four bugs to fix first** (slice 0):
1. **Text put back from outside.** Read `value` before the guard at `ChatInput.svelte:196-201`, or make `editor` a `$state.raw`. Add a component test that sets `value` after mount and checks the editor text.
2. **Enter erases.** `onSubmit` returns a boolean, and the composer clears only on `true`. Include `models.capabilityIssue` in `sendDisabled`. Put a refused queued message back at the head of the queue.
3. **Blank glyphs.** Draw every composer glyph from the Atlas set (`icons.ts:401-406`). Add `send`, `stop` and `mic` in the same 16-unit, 1.5-stroke style. Register any remaining `ri:` names. Add a vitest that fails on an unregistered `ri:` literal. Configure Iconify with no API provider.
4. **Local stays local.** Store the mode on the chat row (B11).

**Structure.**

```
dock (max-width calc(38rem + 3rem), padding 0 1.5rem 1rem)
├─ tray            queued follow-ups, capability note, reply-to chip      (above the pill)
├─ pill            role=group, aria-label "Message composer"
│   ├─ attachments row  (only when files are staged)
│   └─ input row:  [+] [editor] [mic?] [stop?] [send]
└─ controls row    role=toolbar, aria-label "Turn controls"               (under the pill)
    left:  Auto · Balanced · Ask first
    right: Temporary · not saved | context ring (Q30)
```

**Dimensions.**

| Part | Value | Change |
|---|---|---|
| Pill, one line | 48px (32 button + 2×7 padding + 2×1 border) | same |
| Pill padding | 7px 8px 7px 9px | same |
| Radius, one line | `--radius-full` | was 28px |
| Radius, multi-line or with attachments | `--radius-2xl` (20px) | was a 20px literal |
| Border | 1px `--color-border-strong`; hover 28% foreground mix; focus `--color-primary`; Full access `--color-error` | drop `!important` |
| Shadow | 0 2px 4px foreground at 5% | same |
| Buttons | 32px circles, 4px gap, 44px `::after` under `(pointer: coarse)` | same |
| Glyphs | 16px Atlas | today 15/17/18px |
| Editor | 16/24, min 28px, max 200px; phone max `min(200px, 30% of visual viewport height)` | phone cap new |
| Controls row | margin-top 6px; height 20px desktop, 24px phone | new |
| Space below the pill | desktop 6 + 20 + 16 = 42px (24px today with no mode chip). Phone: see 10.12 | new |

**States.**
- **Empty.** Placeholder "Ask Virtues" in subtle ink. Send is a quiet disc: background `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-foreground) 8%, transparent)` with a subtle glyph.
- **With text.** Send fills with `--color-primary` and the glyph turns `--color-background` over 150ms. `:active` scales to 0.94 over `--motion-press` and releases over `--motion-release`.
- **Multi-line.** Past 34px of content the pill takes `--radius-2xl` and bottom-aligns its buttons. Add `border-radius` to the transition at 300ms (`ChatInput.svelte:471-473` lacks it).
- **Turn running, field empty.** The trailing button becomes Stop: an ink disc in `--color-foreground` with a 9×9px `--color-background` square, radius 2px (Q7). Activation is ignored for 400ms after a send. Title and aria-label: "Stop (Esc)".
- **Turn running, with a draft.** Stop moves left as a ghost circle (32px, 1px `--color-border-strong`, muted glyph). Send stays primary and queues the draft. Stop enters with opacity 0→1 and scale 0.85→1 over 150ms. Today Stop is hidden whenever a draft exists (`ChatInput.svelte:156-166`).
- **Queued follow-ups.** A 12px subtle label: "Queued. Sends when this reply finishes." Chip text shows link labels only (composer-24). Clicking a chip puts it back in the composer. Each chip has a "Send now" TextAction, which stops the turn and sends the chip.
- **Editing the last message.** The bubble being edited dims to 0.5 so you can see which one it is. A 12px tray row reads "Editing your message · Cancel". Sending regenerates from that point (versions in 10.9).
- **Temporary chat.** Dashed border as today. The controls row's right end reads "Temporary · not saved". The placeholder stays "Ask Virtues".
- **Full access.** Error border. The Access segment reads "Full access" in `--color-error` at 500, after a 12px Atlas `terminal` glyph. Placeholder: "What should I do on your server?"
- **Icon swap.** The outgoing glyph goes to opacity 0 at once. The incoming glyph goes from 0 to 1 opacity and from scale 0.85 to 1 over 150ms on `--ease-premium`. No rotation and no overshoot (today `ChatInput.svelte:693-710`). Under reduced motion, opacity only over 120ms.
- **Focus retention.** Send, Stop, + and the mic all call `preventDefault()` on `pointerdown`. Call `editor.focus()` after any + menu pick, file pick or controls pick (composer-7).
- **Mentions in the composer.** Drawn as the ref pill (9.2). A plain click selects the node. ⌘-click opens the record beside the chat. The hover card appears after 400ms with Open only. "Turn into…" is dropped (composer-19).
- **Offline.** The tray note reads "Can't reach your server. Messages send when it answers." Messages sent while offline join the queue as chips reading "Will send when your server answers." Fix the false comment at `ComposerTray.svelte:248-249` (composer-23).
- **Enter.** Enter sends when input came from a physical keyboard and makes a new line from a touch keyboard. Decide by input modality: a keydown carrying a physical `code` before any `beforeinput`, or `(hover: none) and (pointer: coarse)`. Today it is decided by window width (`mobileLayout.svelte.ts:67`; composer-6).

**Attachments row (inside the pill).**
- Gap 8px, padding-bottom 6px, scrolls horizontally past the pill's width.
- **Images.** 56px square thumbs, `--radius-md`. The filename goes in the tooltip and aria-label only. Clicking a thumb opens MediaLightbox.
- **Files.** 56px chip: a 32px glyph tile on `--hover-bg`, name 13/18 with ellipsis (max 11rem), meta 12/16 subtle ("PDF · 1.2 MB").
- **Remove.** A 20px circle on `--color-background` with a 1px `--color-border` border and a 12px Atlas `close` glyph, offset −6px from the top-right corner. Shown on hover or focus-within, always under a coarse pointer. Hit area 24px, or 44px coarse. aria-label "Remove {filename}". Today the target is about 17px (composer-14).
- **Reading state.** The chip appears at once on `--hover-bg`. Its content fades in over 150ms once the file is read.
- **Types.** Accept `.gpx` as XML text now. ZIP, XLSX and FIT wait on B26.

**Long paste.**
- Plain text over 4,000 characters or 60 lines becomes a file. Today the threshold is 1,500 characters (`composer.ts:40, 288-291`).
- The chip shows the first line (13px), then "Pasted text · 84 lines". Clicking it opens a read-only sheet with the full text and "Put it in the message".
- For 5 seconds after converting, a 12px TextAction under the chip offers "Paste as text".
- When text is cut at 100 KB, the meta says so: "Sends the first 100 KB of 240 KB."
- In the sent bubble, pasted text draws as an inset block: the first 4 lines at 15/22 in foreground ink, then "Show all 38 lines".

**Dictation.**
- **Mac.** The mic is a 16px Atlas glyph between the editor and Send. `startDictation` is fire-and-forget (`bridge.ts:777-786`). A listening state needs a new shell surface that emits start and stop (inferred feasible). With that surface, the glyph turns `--color-primary` and pulses opacity 1→0.6 on the mark's 1.6s period, and the placeholder reads "Listening". Dictated text never sends on its own.
- **iPhone.** The keyboard's own mic.
- **Browsers.** None. Chrome's Web Speech API sends audio to Google.

**@ mentions.**
- **Typing.** The query is typed inline after `@`, and focus stays in the editor. The trigger stays open across one space while results still match, so "@David Ok" works (`triggers.ts:60-72`). Escape closes the picker and leaves "@query" as typed text.
- **Panel.**
  - Anchored at the `@` through FloatingContent with flip and shift. 320px wide, max-height 320px.
  - One ranked list with a 16px type glyph per row and no group bands. This fixes composer-9, where the arrow order did not match the drawn order.
  - Rows are 36px (44px coarse), padding 6px 8px, gap 8px, `--radius-md`. The selected row gets `--hover-bg`.
  - Title 14px: matched characters in foreground, the rest muted, no bold. Meta 12px subtle, for example "Page · Tue" or "Person".
  - Debounce requests by 100ms and tag each with an incrementing id; drop stale responses. Keep the last rows on screen while loading. After each arrow move, `scrollIntoView({block:'nearest'})` (composer-10, bug-19).
- **Kinds.** Today: person, place, org, file, page, chat, project. Add day, year and source, which already parse as ref routes (`refRoutes.ts:11-25`) and open in tabs. Then:
  - A query that parses as a date ("today", "yesterday", "oct 14", "2025") puts the matching day or year first.
  - A range ("last week", "last spring") inserts plain text with the range resolved, for example "last week (Oct 12 to 18)". So does a chapter ("The city years (2002 to now)"). Neither has a viewer, so neither becomes a chip.
  - A measure ("sleep") offers its source ("Apple Health").
  - Parsing runs on the device, with no model call.
- **Ranking.** Date match first, then exact name or alias, then prefix, then contains. Within each tier: mentioned in this chat, then visit frecency (`GET /api/visits/frecency`; the score is never shown), then people by recent contact, then `updated_at`. Limit 15.
- **Empty query.** One 11px subtle header "Recent", then Today and Yesterday, up to 5 people recently in touch, and up to 5 pages or chats by frecency.
- **Preview.** On desktop, keeping a row selected for 300ms shows a 260px RefCard beside the panel when there is room. No preview on phones.
- **Range echo.** When a time mention resolved, a 12/16 muted line sits under the sent bubble, for example "Read as June 1 to August 31, 2025". Clicking it reopens the picker.
- **Copy.** Placeholder "Mention a person, place, page or day". Empty state "No matches". Picker rows share one recipe with SlashMenu: `--hover-bg` for selection, `--shadow-float`, and a reduced-motion rule (composer-18).

**/ commands.**
- **Trigger.** `/` opens the menu only at offset 0 of the message's first block, so paths and "and/or" never open it. The query is typed inline (`triggers.ts:20-27`). With no match the menu hides and the text stays literal.
- **Menu.**
  - 288px wide, at most 8 rows visible, anchored at the caret.
  - Rows 32px (44px coarse): a 16px glyph, a 13px label, a 12px subtle description on the same line with ellipsis, and a shortcut slot at the right.
  - 11px subtle group headers.
  - With an empty query, the five commands this viewer uses most come first (counted in localStorage).
- **Skills.** A skill inserts a command token at the start of the message, and the placeholder after it becomes the skill's hint. A message that holds only a token can be sent. In the bubble the token draws as a 12px subtle label above the text. On the wire it is `skill: "<name>"`, placed in the uncached tail of the prompt (`skills.rs:1-25`). No skill files exist yet (`skills.rs:55`).

| Command | Kind | What it does | Needs |
|---|---|---|---|
| /week | skill | Look back at the last 7 days: prose first, a figure only when the rows support one | SKILL.md: `sql_query`, `semantic_search`, `show` |
| /chart | skill | One `show` figure and one sentence | SKILL.md |
| /check-in | skill | Progress and setbacks since the last check-in, in words, with no day counts or streaks | SKILL.md |
| /write-up | skill | A first-person page draft you edit before it saves | SKILL.md: `create_page` |
| /web | skill | Web only; the turn gets no record tools, so nothing from your records is sent | SKILL.md |
| /image | skill | Makes an image (asks first under Ask first) | SKILL.md |
| /browse | skill | Uses your Mac's browser; listed only while the Mac app is connected | SKILL.md |
| /task | action | Adds the text as a task, with no model call | `POST /api/tasks` exists |
| /remember | action | Saves the text as a memory note, then shows it with Edit and Undo, with no model call | B15; Q22 |
| /deep, /quick | control | Sets Depth for this message | 10.4; Quick after B18 |
| /model | control | Opens the Model menu | 10.4 |
| /temporary | action | Makes an empty chat temporary | existing toggle |
| /context | action | Opens the context view | existing |
| /compact | action | Summarizes earlier messages; listed above 20% usage | existing |
| /copy, /retry | action | Copy the last reply; try another answer | existing |

The result card for `/task` and `/remember` adds a 12/16 subtle line: "Saved on your server. Nothing was sent to a model." `/skip-onboarding` stays unlisted until Q23 decides the rooms, and is deleted with them. The ∴ / "therefore" easter egg stays outside the menu.

### 10.4 Turn controls and the keyboard map

**How three controls sit on four shapes.**
- The row shows Model, Depth and Access. Each combination maps to one of today's server shapes in `ChatMode`: Chat, Deep research, Sudo and Local (`chat_mode.rs:158-266`).
- Combinations the server cannot honor are unreachable. While Deep research is armed, Access reads "Reads only" and does nothing. A chat with turns from the small NPU model locks the whole row.
- Quick depth waits on the TurnShape split (B18).
- Q33 asks whether Deep research should instead become a skill reached from `/deep`, which is the owner's recorded direction. If yes, the Depth segment drops to Quick and Balanced.

**The controls row.**
- **Text.** "Auto · Balanced · Ask first" at 12/16 Avenir 400 in `--color-foreground-subtle`. A non-default value turns foreground at 500. Full access is the only value with a semantic color. Raise the subtle token first (slice 1).
- **Alignment.** The first letter lines up with the + glyph's left edge, 18px from the pill's outer edge.
- **Segments.** Each is a button: padding 2px 4px, margin 0 −4px, `--radius-sm`. Hover: muted ink on `--hover-bg` over `--motion-hover-in`, back over `--motion-hover-out`. Focus-visible: 2px `--color-border-focus` at 1px offset. The "·" separators are `aria-hidden`, at 60% opacity with 6px margins.
- **Model label.** "Auto", or the catalog `displayName` without the provider, max 10rem. The tooltip on Auto: "Auto uses {displayName} in this chat." No model id literal appears in code.
- **Right end.** The context ring, if Q30 keeps it: 14px, 1.5px stroke, hidden below 50%, subtle ink, `--color-warning` and `--color-error` at the server's `context_status`. Title: "62% of this chat's context. Open the details." Clicking it opens the context view. Then the Temporary caption when it applies.
- **ARIA.** `role="toolbar"` with a roving tabindex: one tab stop; ←/→ between segments; Enter, Space or ↓ opens the menu; inside a menu, ←/→ moves to the neighboring menu. The editable gets `aria-describedby` pointing at an sr-only span that lists only non-default state.
- **Visibility.** Q6 asks whether the row is always visible or reserved but empty until a value differs from the default.

**Model menu** (Q34 asks whether this menu exists at all, or whether the model shows only when it differs from the slot).
- A 280px popover with 32px rows (44px coarse): 13px label, 12px description underneath.
  1. **Auto** ✓. "Uses {Standard display name}. Deep research uses {Deep display name}." Both come from slot resolution, and Auto honors the owner's pin.
  2. **Recent picks**, at most 3, each with its provider in 12px subtle at the right and a retention line from `zdr` (B13): "Doesn't keep your messages" for `all`, "May keep your messages" for `none`, nothing for `some` or unknown.
  3. **On your server.** A BYO endpoint labeled with the owner's credential name ("Your own endpoint. Full tools and records."). On NPU boxes and in an empty chat only: "Small model on this server" ("Can't read your records or use tools.").
  4. **All models…** opens a 320px filterable list of enabled, tool-capable models.
- Footer, 12px subtle: "Applies from your next message."
- **Small-model lock.** A chat with turns from the NPU model shows "Small model on your server · Can't read your records" in the row. Its menu has one row, "Continue in a new chat". A cloud chat can never switch to the NPU model.
- **Invisible switches.** When the capability banner or the error card switches the model, the Model segment shows the new name in foreground ink with a "Reset" item in the menu (composer-12).

**Depth menu.**

| Row | Description | Slot key | Server shape |
|---|---|---|---|
| Quick | "A short answer. A few lookups at most." | 1 | after B18: Thinking off or low, 8 steps, $0.75, 3 min, web cap 2 |
| Balanced ✓ | "Looks things up in your records and the web as needed." | 2 | today's Chat: 20 steps, $2.50, 8 min (`chat_mode.rs:179-185`) |
| Deep research | "Spends several minutes on your records and the web, then writes a report to a page." | 3 | today's DeepResearch: 50 steps, $10, 25 min, Deep slot |

Deep research applies to the next message only, then the row returns to Balanced (Q5). A follow-up should not inherit a $10 ceiling, and every switch to the Deep slot starts a cold prompt cache. While armed, the placeholder reads "What should I look into?"

**Access menu.**

| Row | Description |
|---|---|
| Ask first ✓ | "Asks before running applets, writing to your records or making images." |
| Full access… | "Runs commands on your server without asking. Deleting still asks." |

Choosing "Full access…" replaces the menu with a confirm panel:
- Title (13/20, 500): "Give full access for this chat?"
- Body (12/16 muted): "Your assistant can run commands and change your records on your server without asking. Deleting anything still asks. Full access ends when you leave this chat."
- Buttons: Cancel (quiet, focused first) and "Turn on" (danger outline).
- The announcer says "Full access is on for this chat."

Full access is in no cycle and has no chord.

| Action | Ask first | Full access |
|---|---|---|
| Read records, `show`, `web_search`, `code_interpreter` | runs | runs |
| `create_page`, `create_task`, `update_memory`, `edit_page` | runs; the record offers Undo where page versions allow | runs |
| `send_imessage` | runs, with the existing 5s undo card | same; never bypassed |
| `sql_write`, run/setup/edit applet | asks (applet rows depend on Q21) | runs |
| `delete_applet`, `generate_image` | asks | asks |
| SQL DROP/TRUNCATE/DELETE, destructive shell | no shell offered | asks, showing the exact command |
| `shell` | not offered | runs |

Change the Chat description today, "Asks before changing anything" (`agentModes.ts:34`), which is false because `edit_page` runs without asking. Delete the `prompt.rs:100` line about `edit_page` returning `permission_needed` (knows-3).

**Defaults, persistence and wire.**
- A new chat starts as Auto · Balanced · Ask first.
- Model and Depth are stored on the chat row at first send and returned with the chat (B11).
- Access stays view state and resets when the view moves to another chat. This keeps the rule at `ChatView.svelte:636-638`.
- Wire: `{ model: 'auto' | id | 'byo:<cred>' | 'npu', depth: 'quick' | 'balanced' | 'deep', access: 'ask' | 'full', skill?: name }`.
- **Backward compatibility.** The server keeps accepting the old `agent_mode` field and maps it: chat → balanced + ask; deep_research → deep; sudo → balanced + full; local → npu. For a chat stored as local, the server runs the turn locally whatever an older client sends and reports the effective mode in start metadata (B1). It does not refuse. That keeps the privacy promise without tightening a request field, which the owner's notes forbid.
- Move mode and depth guidance out of the cached `base` block into a tail block (B30). Always declare `browse` and `send_imessage` in chat and refuse them with a tool result when the Mac app is disconnected, so connecting does not change the cache key (B31). Remove the dead `persona` and `agentId: 'auto'` fields (B28). The `'auto'` value would otherwise collide with the new Auto model.

**Keyboard map.**

| Keys | Where | Action | Today |
|---|---|---|---|
| Enter | composer, physical keyboard | send | decided by width; change to input modality |
| Enter | composer, touch keyboard | new line | same |
| Shift+Enter | composer | line break, list, quote, fence | same |
| Mod+Enter | composer | send from anywhere | same |
| Tab / Shift+Tab | composer | indent or outdent in a list or code; otherwise move focus | Shift+Tab cycles mode: removed (Q4) |
| Esc | composer | 1) close the picker or menu, 2) stop a running turn and keep the draft, 3) blur | 1 and 3 exist |
| ⌘. | anywhere in the chat tab | stop the turn | new |
| ↑ | empty composer, empty new chat with an opening | thread the opening sentence | new |
| ↑ | empty composer, chat with turns, no turn running | edit the last message you sent | new |
| ⌥↑ / ⌥↓ | empty composer, or focus outside text | ⌥↑ from the composer focuses the newest reply; further ⌥↑ moves to earlier turns; ⌥↓ moves later; past the last it returns to the composer | new |
| / | chat tab, focus outside any field | focus the composer and open the slash menu | new |
| ⌘⇧M | chat tab | open the Model menu (←/→ to Depth and Access) | new |
| 1 / 2 / 3 | open Depth menu | Quick / Balanced / Deep research | new |
| ⌘/ | anywhere | shortcut sheet rendered from `shortcuts.all` | new |

**Transcript keys.** With focus on a turn (each turn is an `<article tabindex="-1">`, 10.13), bare keys work: J/K next and previous turn, Y copy the reply body, R try another answer (last reply only), E edit (last user message only), O open the first figure's rows, Enter open "Worked for", Esc return to the composer.

**Palette.** ⌘K gets a "This chat" group at the top while a chat is focused: Stop, Copy last reply, Try another answer, Context, Turns in this chat, Depth. Change palette rows to sentence case: "New chat", "Go to wiki", "Change theme" (`SearchModal.svelte:99-162`).

**Conflicts checked.**
- None of the new chords collide with the app's registrations (`UnifiedSidebar.svelte:70-131`; `(app)/+layout.svelte:243-264`) or with the Mac summon chord, CmdOrCtrl+Shift+Space.
- ⌘⇧M opens Chrome's profile menu and Firefox's responsive design mode in a browser tab (from memory). It is free in Tauri. The menu is one Tab away anyway.
- ⌘. may mean "stop loading" in Safari (from memory). Esc covers that case.
- Hide hints for chords the browser keeps on the web: ⌘N, ⌘⇧N, ⌘⇧T, ⌘W.
- Option+digit chords were rejected because they type brackets on several Mac layouts.
- In the Mac app (no menu bar, `main.rs:1953-1956`), ⌘W closes the active tab and the wiki moves to ⌘⇧W (Q19).
- Each chord is registered through `shortcuts.register` and shown through `shortcuts.format()`. Today `ChatInput.svelte:433, 440` hard-codes "Shift+Tab".

**Composer status region.** One sr-only `role="status"` inside the dock, mounted empty. It announces: "Model: {name}", "Deep research for this message", "Full access is on for this chat", "Queued. Sends when this reply finishes.", "Pasted text added as a file", "Stopped", "Copied". The pill becomes `role="group" aria-label="Message composer"` with no tabindex. The editable gets `aria-placeholder`. The static `id="chat-input"` goes (composer-16, a11y-17).

### 10.5 The new chat

**Options weighed.**
- **Widgets as briefed.** Rejected for the reasons in 7.1.
- **A blank composer.** Rejected. It drops the one thing only Virtues can do.
- **Today's sky plus one sentence.** Kept as the base. The sentence is judged by the model, can say NONE, cannot be forged by the client, and becomes turn one. Its weaknesses are fixable: dot height is a hash, nothing in the sky can be clicked, and the sentence attaches to any first message.

**Layout (desktop).** One hero slot for every empty state, anchored at `bottom: calc(50% + 64px)`. The temporary and local heroes use the same slot. It is a centered column `min(560px, 100%)` wide, stacked top to bottom:

1. **"Needs you"** (Q32). 13/20 muted sans with a 6px `--color-primary` dot: "A chat is waiting for you: Kitchen tile quote · Open". Shown only when a turn is paused for an approval or a browser handoff. It comes from turn state, not from model judgment, and needs the your-turn state from slice 2.
2. **The sky** (viewBox 560×120).
   - Dots pack into up to 3 lanes 14px apart. A lone event sits on a 1px `--color-border-subtle` baseline.
   - Hour labels "6 AM", "Noon", "6 PM" are HTML at 11px tabular subtle sans, positioned by percentage.
   - A "now" tick: 1px `--color-primary` at 60%, with a 4px dot on the baseline.
   - Calendar events later today draw as hollow 1px rings, from `/api/calendar/upcoming`.
   - Every dot is ink 0.55, and the halo on the event the sentence names is the only emphasis. This drops the fixed 0.9 novelty ink, which `agents/plan/attention-plan.md:309-321` defends (Q8).
   - The halo is static at stop-opacity 0.22 and breathes once on load. The twinkle loop and the 4.8s breathing loop go (start-9).
   - Dot radius scales by 560 / rendered width so dots stay 1.3–4.6px on screen.
   - Hover (desktop) or tap (phone) a dot for a 13px card: "2:30 to 3:15 PM · Dentist · Elm St", with times in the owner's locale and an "Ask about this" TextAction. Clicking the dot threads that event.
3. **The sentence**, 16px below the sky. Serif 1.125rem/1.625rem (18/26), weight 400, `--color-foreground`, max-width 32em, `text-wrap: balance`, centered. It is a button: on hover, a dotted underline at 45% currentColor, offset 4px. aria-label "Reply to this". It sits in a polite live region and is announced once.
4. **Also lines** (Q10). 0–2 lines, 8px below, 4px apart. Sans 14/20 muted, centered, each a button. Backend: `chat_opening` returns up to three `[n]`-tagged lines or NONE, keeps the 40-word cap, and stays on the Standard slot.
5. **Provenance.** A reserved 16px row. It fades in over 150ms on hover or focus of any line. 12/16 subtle: "Written by your assistant from today's events, on {Standard display name}." With local AI preferred: "Written on your server" (Q25).

**Rules from the recovery and data-sovereign reviews.**
- The opening prompt receives the owner's active `avoid` rules: "Never open with anything that touches these." NONE stays the expected answer when an avoided subject leads the day (B19). The halo follows the sentence's `event_id`, so a NONE draws no halo.
- A setting, "Open new chats with a line about your day", defaults to on. It lives in Settings › Assistant.
- When `getDayEvents` fails it must report the failure separately from an empty day (`wiki/api.ts:1136-1143`; start-21). The fallback is the ∴ mark.
- `notifications-plan.md:146, 220, 243-244` must stop naming the removed Home as the destination for "Needs you".

**Threading** (Q31).
- A line threads only when chosen: a click on a line or a dot, or ↑ in an empty composer, which threads the sentence.
- Threading puts a reply-to chip in the tray: "Replying to: Dentist on Elm St at 2:30…". Removing it un-threads.
- On send, only a threaded line is stored as the first assistant turn (`store_opening`). With nothing threaded, nothing is stored. Today the opening is attached to any first send, including when attachments hid the hero (`ChatView.svelte:1663-1686`).
- A stored opening renders as an ordinary assistant turn in 18/26 serif.

**Collapse on send.**

| Time | What moves |
|---|---|
| 0–200ms | Unthreaded hero parts: opacity 1→0, translateY 0→−8px, `--ease-premium` |
| 0–400ms | The composer dock glides to the bottom, transform only, on `cubic-bezier(0.76,0,0.24,1)`. Today it animates `bottom`, a layout property (`ChatView.svelte:2979-2986`) |
| 0–400ms | The threaded line translates from its hero rect to turn one's rect (FLIP, same curve). From 280 to 400ms it fades out while the stored copy fades in from 240 to 400ms. Centered and left-aligned text break lines differently, so a pure FLIP would jump at the end |
| 100–500ms | The user bubble enters with the transcript reveal, retimed to the same curve |

Under reduced motion each step is a 150ms opacity crossfade with no travel. One exit applies to the temporary and local heroes too (start-12). Fix the "~600ms" comment at `ChatView.svelte:2980`.

**Phone.**
- The composer is not auto-focused on a new chat (mobile-4).
- The hero centers in the space above the docked composer.
- When the keyboard opens, the sky and the also lines fade over 200ms. The sentence collapses to one line (15px serif, ellipsis) pinned 12px under the bar. The "Needs you" line takes that slot instead when present.

**Arrival.** The dots' 0.6s arrival (`cubic-bezier(0.22,1,0.36,1)`, 45ms stagger, capped at 1200ms) plays once per part of the day. After that the sky is drawn at rest (Q35).

**Fallback mark.** Draw the empty-day ∴ with RestingMark's geometry (viewBox −3 −3 30 30, r 3, 20px). Today `NewChatSky.svelte:110-113` uses its own non-equilateral triangle. Drop the 0.2px text-stroke on "Virtues" (`NewChatSky.svelte:167`), which thickens the serif. Set the word at `--md-h2-size`.

**Accessible name.** Build it from the data, for example "Today: 6 events so far, 2 still to come. Your assistant mentions the 2:30 PM dentist on Elm St." Announce it once.

### 10.6 One stylesheet: type, spacing, color

`prose.css` already draws replies, pages, articles and the composer (`prose.css:17-22`), and `prose.test.ts` (1d326bdfb) fails any text style written outside it. This section adds one modifier, `.prose--reply`, beside `.prose--article` and `.prose--compact`. The modifier only redefines tokens. Two commits after 4ceea332c changed heading sizes (9c6031ddd "headings scale with the page's text size", dee0fe1d8 "an article's lower headings step above its body text"). Re-check the type table against them before building.

**Grounds and rules.**

| Theme | `--prose-ground` | `--prose-rule` | muted text on ground | subtle text on ground |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| Oxford (default light) | #F4F4F5, 1.10:1 | #E8E9EA, 1.22:1 | 8.78 | 5.11 |
| Pemberley | #F3F3F4, 1.11:1 | #E8E8E8, 1.23:1 | 9.42 | 4.36 |
| Asgard (working tree) | #202021, 1.10:1 | #2A2A2B, 1.25:1 | 4.65 | 3.05 |
| Asgard (committed) | #252629, 1.11:1 | #2E2F32, 1.26:1 | 4.33 | 2.84 |

- Text on `--prose-ground` is foreground or muted, never subtle.
- Asgard's muted ink also needs raising before muted text on a ground clears AA at committed values.
- Applying a ground on Pemberley overrides its stated flat-white intent (Q38).

**Type scale.** `.prose` gets `font-synthesis-weight: none`, so nothing is faked bold. Avenir ships 400, 500 and 700. EB Garamond ships 300–400 only (`app.css:86-178`).

| Element | Sans (chat, sans pages) | Article (wiki, serif pages) | Space above / below | Change |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| body | Avenir 16/24 | Garamond 17/28 | gap 1em | article line-height 1.65 (28.05px) → 28px |
| h1 | Garamond 26/32, 400 | same | 40 / 8 | lh 1.3 → 32px |
| h2 | Garamond 22/28, 400 | same | 32 / 8 | lh 1.35 → 28px |
| h3 | Garamond 18/24, 400 | same | 24 / 8 | weight 500 → 400 |
| h4 | Avenir 16/24, 500 | Garamond 17/28, 400 | 24 / 8 | check against dee0fe1d8 |
| h5 | Avenir 15/24, 500 | Garamond 17/28, 400, muted | 20 / 8 | 600 drew Avenir Bold or faux-bold serif (type-2, type-3) |
| h6 | Avenir 14/20, 500, muted | Garamond 17/28, 400, muted | 20 / 8 | same |
| table cell | `--md-cell-size` (15/22 at default) | 16 at the article size | — | derives from `--prose-size`, so S and L reach cells (type-18) |
| th | Avenir 13/20, 400, muted | same | — | no ground; `--md-th-weight: 400` clears the TODO at `prose.css:352` |
| inline code | Plex Mono 0.875em | same | — | ground becomes `--prose-ground`; the 1px border goes |
| code block | Plex Mono 14/22 | same | gap 1em | lh 1.6 → 22px |

`--md-cell-size: calc(var(--prose-size, var(--md-body-size)) - 1px)` with `line-height: 1.4667`. That gives whole pixels at the default size (15/22) and accepts fractional lines at S and L.

**`.prose--reply`** caps the h1 at h2's look: `--md-h1-size: var(--md-h2-size); --md-h1-lh: var(--md-h2-lh)`. Markdown depth + 2 becomes the HTML element (`#` → `<h3>`), because each turn gets an sr-only `<h2>` (10.13). Use Streamdown's `heading` snippet (`svelte-streamdown/dist/Elements/Element.svelte:27-33`) with `data-depth`, and select the look by `:is(h1, [data-depth="1"])`.

**Emphasis** (Q12).
- `strong` stays Avenir 500 in sans. In serif contexts: `font-family: var(--font-sans); font-weight: 500; font-size: 0.9375em`. Today it falls back to the 400 cut and the emphasis vanishes.
