- `em`: synthesized oblique in sans. In serif: `font-style: normal`.
- `del, s`: muted ink, `text-decoration-color: currentColor`.
- `sup, sub`: `font-size: 0.75em; line-height: 0`.
- `kbd` (new): Avenir 0.8125em, padding `0 0.35em`, 1px `--prose-rule`, `--radius-sm`.
- `dl`: `dt` in foreground at 500; `dd` indented by `var(--prose-indent)`.

**Blocks.**
- **Lists.** `::marker { color: var(--color-foreground-muted) }`. Ordered markers use tabular figures. Item gap `0.375em` (6px).
- **Quotes.** 3px `--color-border` bar, muted ink. `padding-left: calc(var(--prose-indent) - 3px)`, so quote text and list text share a 24px edge. A quote with a source line gets a different style (10.9).
- **Code blocks** (`.prose-code`, shared with pages; transcript-23).
  - Frame: 1px `--prose-rule`, `--radius-xl`, `--prose-ground`.
  - Header: `min-height: 32px; padding: 0 4px 0 12px`. The language label is 12px muted in display names. With no language there is no label (today "Plain text", `MarkdownCodeBlock.svelte:69`), and Copy stays at the right as `IconButton sm` (24px).
  - Copy is disabled while the fence is still open. "Copied" goes to the announcer.
  - Highlighting stays deferred to the end of the turn (`MarkdownCodeBlock.svelte:8-18`).
  - Any change here also changes pages' code blocks.
- **Tables.** One open design for every table in a reply. ShowTable's style becomes the prose table:

  ```css
  .prose table { border: none; border-radius: 0; border-collapse: collapse; width: 100%; }
  .prose th, .prose td { padding: 6px 12px 6px 0; border: none; border-bottom: 1px solid var(--prose-rule);
    font-size: var(--md-cell-size); line-height: 1.4667; font-variant-numeric: tabular-nums lining-nums; }
  .prose th { font-size: var(--md-th-size); line-height: 20px; font-weight: var(--md-th-weight);
    color: var(--color-foreground-muted); background: none; border-bottom-color: var(--color-border); padding-bottom: 8px; }
  .prose tr:last-child > td { border-bottom: none; }
  ```

  Wide tables keep `.tableWrapper { overflow-x: auto }`. This removes the square header fill showing past the rounded frame (type-12) and adds tabular figures (type-13). Pages get the same unless Q14 keeps them framed. If they do get it, the page editor draws cell guides in `document.css` only while editing.
- **Rules (`hr`).** The line moves to `--prose-rule`.
- **Images.** Every picture in text uses `--radius-md` (8px), including message images and generated images, and every picture opens the lightbox from a wrapping `<button aria-label="Open image: {name}">`.
- **Callouts.** `--prose-ground` with a `--prose-rule` border. Tip and warning keep their semantic subtle fills.
- **Math.**
  - Add `katex` to `apps/web/package.json`; it is not installed today. Import `svelte-streamdown/math` and pass `components={{ math: StreamdownMath }}`.
  - Check that KaTeX's fonts ship as self-hosted woff2 under the Tauri CSP.
  - Sizes: `.prose .katex { font-size: 1.1em; color: inherit }`; `.prose--article .katex { font-size: 1.05em }`; `.prose .katex-display { margin: 0; padding: 4px 0; overflow-x: auto }`; block math sits on `--prose-gap`.
  - `Math.svelte` keeps the last good render while TeX is still incomplete.
  - KaTeX's italic variables are a serif italic (Q13).
- **Selection.** `::selection` uses `--selection`. `mark` keeps `--color-highlight`. Find matches use primary at 10% with the current match outlined (type-14).

**Contrast fixes** (slice 1; type-8, type-9, type-10, type-24, a11y-9). Every candidate value is chosen by a contrast script against page, surface and surface-elevated, and must reach at least 4.5:1 for text.
- Raise `--color-foreground-subtle` in Asgard, Lothlorien, Lyceum, Tatooine and Agora (they fail on the page) and in Caladan, Rivendell, Netherfield and Hogwarts (they fail on surface-elevated). Do not use the three replacement values the verify pass found failing.
- Fix `--primary` as text in Pemberley (3.65:1) and Tatooine (3.41:1), keeping hover darker than rest and `--border-focus` equal to `--primary`.
- Set Baker Street's muted ink to a real step below foreground.
- Make Canterbury's `--border-subtle` lighter than its page.
- Draw the to-do box outline in subtle ink.
- In Asgard, keep page at or above surface with a desk step of at least 1.08:1 (type-25).

**Streaming.**
- The word fade becomes 200ms with `timingFunction: 'cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0, 1)'`. Today it is 300ms on Streamdown's default ease-in (`Markdown.svelte:139-145`).
- Turn off the block-level fade so only words fade (transcript-18).
- Client-side pacing of bursts (in the style of smoothStream) is not adopted for now. The word fade already spreads arrival over 200ms, and a pacer can build a backlog. Revisit only if bursts look jumpy on a phone.

**Reduced motion.**
- Use one helper. Svelte's `prefersReducedMotion` from `svelte/motion` (already used in `HomePanel.svelte:47` and `ActivityHeatmap.svelte:24`) is reactive. `setup/motion.ts:15` keeps its `reducedMotion()` name and reads `prefersReducedMotion.current`. The private copy in `document/drag-handle.ts:138` and the other hand-rolled `matchMedia` checks (about 17) move onto it in the same change.
- Gaps to fix with it (motion-10): the CitationPanel slide and backdrop, the CitationTooltip rise, RefPreview's rise, the composer Spring (`ChatInput.svelte:140`; set it with `{instant: true}`), the send swap, SubagentPanel's spin (deleted in slice 2), every `scrollTo({behavior:'smooth'})`, and the UserMessage slide.

**Forced colors.** Add `@media (forced-colors: active)` rules (motion-25):
- Rail marks drawn with `border-top: 2px solid CanvasText`.
- The composer focus border in `Highlight`.
- Chart series kept apart by dash and hatch.

**Appearance.** Add a "Match system" theme choice that applies `DEFAULT_THEMES.light` or `.dark` (`theme.ts:58-61`) from `prefers-color-scheme` and updates on change (type-25).

**Reading settings for chat.**
- Chat gains the page reading settings per viewer: Sans or Serif, S/M/L, using `pageDisplay.svelte.ts:23-57`. Serif sets replies in `.prose--article`. Your own bubble stays in sans.
- In Serif, cap text blocks at 34rem inside the 38rem column. Figures, tables and code keep the full width.
- Add `text-wrap: pretty` on `.prose p` if the Tauri WebKit supports it, and `hyphens: auto` on English prose in Serif only, after a WebKit check (slice 8).

**Chrome token fixes** (type-21, consistency-24, reply-ui-13).
- `.message-action` uses `--color-*` spellings and `--hover-bg`.
- `--color-text` becomes `--color-foreground`. `--color-surface-hover` becomes `--hover-bg`.
- Remove the `rgba(var(--…-rgb), …)` fallbacks.
- Fonts use `--font-mono`.
- Extend `tools/design-lint.sh` to reject these spellings. Cut its baseline in a scratch clone (`git clone --local -b wave . /tmp/baseline-check`), never in the shared checkout.

**Docs pass** (type-22). Correct the `themes.css:187-195` comment, `Markdown.svelte:111-113`, `typography.md:53-56` (600 and above is synthesized unless synthesis is off), design.md's tone table and Type row, and design-grammar §4's scale.

### 10.7 Reply primitives

**Two rules.**
1. A framed block is something you act on. An unframed block reports what the assistant did. This follows design-grammar §2 and §6.
2. The reply is styled only through tokens (10.6).

**ReplyCard** (framed). Built on `Card.svelte` (1px `--color-border`, `--radius-xl`, `--color-surface`, 16px padding; `Card.svelte:57-70`).

```
ReplyCard
  margin: var(--prose-gap) 0;  max-width: 100% of the column
  .head    13/20 sans, --color-foreground-muted, min-height 20px, flex, gap 8px
           optional trailing IconButton sm (24px): open the thing
  .body    margin-top 8px; rows 15/22 sans foreground, or 16/24 serif for the owner's words
  .actions margin-top 12px; flex, gap 8px; Act pills (primary = ink fill, quiet = hairline)
  role="group", aria-labelledby=.head
```

- No shadow and no colored border. Danger is shown once, on the button.
- Below a 480px container, actions stack full width, each at least 44px tall, 12px apart.
- **Settling.** When the action is done, the card turns into a record. Border and ground fade to transparent over 300ms on `--ease-premium`, with padding unchanged so nothing below moves. Then it swaps to a ReplyRecord. Under reduced motion it swaps at once. The CSS is in Appendix A.

**ReplyRecord** (unframed). Built like the "Worked for" header.

```
ReplyRecord
  <button> when it has a fold, else <div>; inline-flex, baseline, gap 6px
  13/20 sans, --color-foreground-muted; padding 4px 12px; margin-left -12px (hangs on the text edge)
  radius-full; hover --hover-bg over --motion-hover-in, out over --motion-hover-out
  trailing chevron 12px, stroke 1.25, rotates 90° over --motion-state when open
  fold: margin-top 8px, padding 12px 16px, --hover-bg, --radius-lg, 13/1.6
  aria-expanded; :focus-visible 2px --color-border-focus, offset 2px
  (pointer: coarse): padding 12px so the target clears 44px
```

A run of records is spaced 8px above the first and below the last, with no space between them.

**Which block becomes what.** Move all eleven blocks in one change and delete the hand-rolled boxes, per CLAUDE.md's "a better way replaces the old way".

| Block today | Becomes | Copy and behavior |
|---|---|---|
| `TaskCard` | ReplyCard | Head "Added 3 tasks" as a TextAction to Tasks. Rows 15/22 with an 18px check and a 44px coarse hit area. Chips become 12px muted text; overdue gets a 6px `--color-warning` dot |
| `PageBindingInline` (approvals) | ReplyCard | 10.8 |
| `AppletProposalCard` | ReplyCard, then a record | "Turn it on" / "Not now" → "Turned on Morning digest · Open". Depends on Q21 |
| `SentMessageCard` | ReplyCard while cancel is possible, then a record | The owner's words in Garamond 16/24, muted until sent. After delivery: "Delivered to Nick · 3:42 PM", with the words below as a quote |
| Browser handoff | ReplyRecord (new) | "Your browser needs you: {reason in full} · Show the browser" on the Mac; "Your browser on your Mac needs you: {reason}" on the phone. "I can't" and "Done" stay in the Browser pane (`BrowserView.svelte:307-313`) |
| First `browser_open` in a chat | ReplyRecord (new) | "Your assistant opened example.com · Show". The window splits only on Show, or if a Browser tab is already open (mobile-11) |
| `CodeInterpreterCard` + `TurnFigures` | Figure(s) at the call's place, plus a record | Plots unframed, `--radius-md`, lightbox on click, alt text from the model's caption, else "Chart from your code". Record: "Ran Python on your server · 1.2s ›". The fold holds Code (mono 12/18) and Output. Failure: "Python stopped with an error · 0.4s ›" in foreground ink with a 6px error dot. Delete `TurnFigures` |
| `EditDiffCard` | ReplyRecord | "Edited Trip notes ›", with the title a TextAction to the page. The fold holds a word-level diff in the page's own face at 15px: `del` with a line-through in subtle, `ins` with a 1px primary underline at 40% |
| `PageEditResult` | ReplyRecord | "Created a page: Trip notes · Open" |
| `CompactionCheckpoint` | ReplyRecord | "Earlier messages summarized · 42 messages ›". The fold holds the summary as 13px prose with XML stripped. No version chip |
| `StoppedNotice` | ReplyRecord | Same eight reasons, for example "Stopped by you · Continue". The dangling-turn row becomes "Your server never finished this one · Try again" |
| Red tool-error box | ReplyRecord | "Couldn't finish reading your messages · Details ›", named through `describeTool(part, true)`. The tool id moves into the fold. Image failure: "Image generation failed. {errorText}" |
| "Generating image…" | Figure placeholder | A square reserved at `min(420px, 100%)`, then the image with the source line "Made by your assistant" |
| Memory write | ReplyRecord | 10.11 |

**Errors.** Keep the ChatError card (`ChatError.svelte`) for failures where no reply arrived. Fix:
- The `??` chain that never reaches its "interrupted" fallback: use `||` (`ChatError.svelte:216-222`).
- Point "Reconnect" and "Connect an AI" at setup's AI step instead of `/`.
- Convert its px sizes to rem.

**Reply layout and rhythm.**

```
┌ column: 38rem measure + 1.5rem gutters ─────────────────────────────┐
│                                       ╭──────────────────────────╮  │
│                              9:41 AM  │ How did I sleep in March? │  │  bubble
│                                       ╰──────────────────────────╯  │
│  24px                                                               │
│  ∴ Worked for 12s · your records ›          13/20 muted, hanging    │
│  12px                                                               │
│  Paragraph 16/24 …                                                  │
│  16px (--prose-gap)                                                 │
│  24px ─ Figure title 18/24 serif                                    │
│         [chart]                                                     │
│         From 31 nights of your records, Mar 1–31 · See the rows     │
│  24px                                                               │
│  Ran Python on your server · 1.2s ›         receipt line, 28px      │
│  8px                                                                │
│  ⧉ ↻ ⋯                                      actions, 28px buttons   │
│  48px                                                               │
│                                        ╭ next question ╮            │
```

- **Between turns.** 24px from a question to its reply and 48px from a reply to the next question (40px on phones), so an exchange reads as a pair (type-17; design-grammar §6). Implement with `.messages-container { gap: 0 }`, `.message-wrapper { padding: 0 }`, `[data-role=user] + .message-wrapper { margin-top: 24px }`, and `:not([data-role=user]) + [data-role=user] { margin-top: 48px }`.
- **Inside a reply.** The thinking block sits 12px above the text. Text blocks are separated by `--prose-gap`. Figures get 24px above and below. A ReplyCard gets 16px.
- **Containment.** `.message-wrapper` becomes `contain: layout style`, dropping `paint`, which clips popovers and the hanging mark (transcript-5). Turns other than the streaming tail get `content-visibility: auto; contain-intrinsic-size: auto 400px` (10.13).

**Choices** (reply-ui-16, mobile-5).
- `Act` quiet pills: 14/1.2, padding 7px 15px, `--radius-full`, 1px `--color-border`, gap 8px, transitions on `--motion-state`.
- Below a 480px container, choices stack as full-width rows: `min-height: 44px; text-align: left; white-space: normal`. A choice that wraps uses `--radius-lg`.
- Once a later user message exists, the row is not drawn, because your bubble directly below is the answer (Q36). This also holds on reload.
- One tab stop into the group, arrows between pills, Enter or Space sends.
- After a tap, focus goes to the composer and the announcer says "Sent: {choice}".

### 10.8 Approvals

**Bugs to fix first.**
- Deny is `() => {}` (`ChatView.svelte:2055`).
- Cards have no `active` gate, so Allow on an old turn acts on the newest.
- Allow posts "Allowed. Run exactly that command." as a user bubble the owner never typed (`ChatView.svelte:333-338`).
- `edit_page`'s `needs_binding` carries no `page_id` (`page_editor.rs:588`).
- B12 fixes all four.

**States.**

| State | Look |
|---|---|
| Pending (newest finished turn, no later user message) | ReplyCard. Head: "{Assistant name} wants to {act}". Body: the target. Actions: primary and "Don't allow" |
| Allowed | Becomes a record: "You allowed this · 9:42 AM ›". The fold holds the exact command |
| Declined | "You didn't allow this · 9:42 AM ›" |
| Not answered (older turn) | "Not answered ›", no buttons |

Deny and Allow travel as a control field in the request, `approval: { entity_id, allow }`, and are stored, so a reload shows the same state. If a message row must exist, it carries a subject and draws as a centered 12/16 subtle line.

**Copy for each gate.** These come from `PERMISSION_REQUIRED` (`executor.rs:291-307`; working tree 297-313) and `sudo_gate.rs:61-76`. Replace the server's "AI wants to {verb} \"{title}\"" (`executor.rs:388`) and the client fallback at `PageBindingInline.svelte:55` at the source. The applet rows depend on Q21, because a 2026-10-06 note says to drop Allow for chat-authored applets.

| Gate | Head | Body | Primary | Secondary |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| run_applet | wants to run an applet | Name as a TextAction | Run it | Don't allow |
| edit_applet | wants to change an applet | Name · "This can change its prompt and schedule." | Allow the change | Don't allow |
| setup_applet | wants to create an applet | Proposed name | Create it | Don't allow |
| delete_applet | wants to delete an applet | Name | Delete it (danger) | Keep it |
| sql_write | wants to change your records | "Rows it deletes can't be brought back from chat." | Allow the change | Don't allow |
| generate_image | wants to make an image | "Each image costs money and sends your prompt to {image provider}." | Make it | Don't allow |
| sudo (SQL, shell) | wants to run this on your server | Command block, plus a kind line ("This deletes data on your server.") | Run it (danger) | Don't run it |

- **Command block.** Plex Mono 13/20 on `--prose-ground`, `--radius-md`, padding 8px 12px, `white-space: pre-wrap`, `max-height: 12rem` with its own scroll, and a copy control. It shows up to the 2,000 characters the gate sends. Today it inherits the sans face (`PageBindingInline.svelte:195-205`).
- **Danger button.** `Act` with a 1px `--color-error` border and error ink, no fill.
- **Keyboard.**
  - No shortcut for Allow.
  - Focus never moves to the card on its own.
  - When focus is inside the card, the buttons work as normal buttons, and Esc means "Don't allow". Only the safe direction has a key.
  - On danger gates, the first focus inside the card lands on the safe button.
  - After a choice, focus returns to the composer.
- **Announcements.** When a card appears: "Your assistant is waiting for you to allow: {first 60 characters}". The paused header shows the still ∴ and "Waiting for you to allow this" (10.1). The sidebar row shows "Waiting for you" in place of the unread dot.
- **Sending while a card waits.** Sending a new message while a card waits counts as "Don't allow". The refusal goes to the box before the message, and the card settles.
- No card ever offers "Always allow" for deletes, sends or Full access.
- **Phone.** Stack below 480px. Buttons are full width and at least 44px tall.

### 10.9 Citations, the user message and message actions

**Bug to fix first** (reply-ui-1). `Markdown.svelte:205-212` tests `isEntity` before `queryCitation`, and `parseEntityRoute` matches any `/chat/` path (`refRoutes.ts:19, 99-109`). Swap the order, and make `parseEntityRoute` refuse a path with a segment after the id. Add a vitest that renders `[31 nights](/chat/chat_a/tool/call_1)` and expects a `.query-ref`. Give `.query-ref` a focus-visible outline (a11y-22).

**Two link styles** (Q11). Both use the text's own ink.

| Kind | Look | Today |
|---|---|---|
| Name: person, place, org, page, chat, project, day | `text-decoration: underline 1px; text-decoration-color: color-mix(in srgb, currentColor 30%, transparent); text-underline-offset: 0.2em`. Hover: primary ink and decoration at 60% over `--motion-hover-in`. No "@" and no icon | Accent, an 11px icon and a literal "@" (`Ref.svelte:65-69`) |
| Source: record, rows behind a figure, web | `underline dotted 1px` at 45% currentColor, offset 0.2em. Hover: solid, primary | Already the quiet style for web and query refs |

- `Ref` renders an `<a href>` with the click intercepted, so long names can break across lines. A `<button>` never breaks.
- The composer keeps its pill mention.
- Whether the day biography's names move to the solid line is part of Q11.

**Source card** (restyles `RefPreview`, which is already portaled to `<body>`).
- **Frame.** `width: min(300px, 100vw - 32px)`, padding 12px 16px, `--radius-xl`, `--color-surface`, 1px `--color-border`, `--shadow-float`, `--z-popover`.
- **Line 1** (12/16 muted, tabular): kind and time, for example "Message · Tue, Mar 3, 9:41 PM", "31 rows · ran on your server at 9:42 AM", or "example.com".
- **Body.** For a record with text: the passage in Garamond 16/24, at most 4 lines, under a 13px muted speaker line. Example: "David Okafor" then "Are we still on for the coast road?" For a name: the title at 17/24 serif and one 13px line. For a web page: its title at 15/22, at most 2 lines.
- **Actions** (13px TextActions, 16px gap): "Open". "See the rows" for rows. "Not the one I meant" for records, which puts "That isn't the record I meant: " into the composer unsent.
- **Motion.** Opacity plus a 3px rise over 180ms on `--ease-premium`. Opacity only under reduced motion.
- **Touch.** The first tap shows the card. "Open" navigates; on the phone it opens the record sheet.
- **Screen readers.** The link carries `aria-describedby` pointing at the passage.
- "Turn into…" is removed from replies.
- **Record summaries.** Add a `record` case to `refSummary` that returns source, author, time and the first three lines. Today `RefCard.svelte:32-44` fetches summaries only for person, place and org.
- **Dead links.** A link whose ref does not resolve renders as plain text with a dotted subtle underline and the tooltip "Your server couldn't find this source" (B27). Today `chat.rs:2549-2557` only logs dead links.
- **Later.** Passage-level record refs with `?q=` (as drive refs have, `semantic_search.rs:170-181`). Grouping three or more adjacent sources as "+2" with a "‹ 1 of 3 ›" card.

**The rows view.** This replaces `CitationPanel` for `show` and `sql_query`. Today the panel titles every query "Tool / Data Source", shows the render time as "Retrieved" and never shows the SQL (reply-ui-9).
- Opens beside the chat on desktop and as a sheet on the phone.
- Title: the figure's title in Garamond 22/28, or "Rows behind this answer".
- Caption (13px muted): "214 rows · ran on your server at 9:42 AM", from the call's own time.
- Body: the shared prose table, columns in SELECT order, `formatCell`, 50 rows a page.
- A disclosure "The query" holds the SQL in Plex Mono 13/20 on `--prose-ground` with Copy. A TextAction "Copy as CSV" follows.
- Close returns focus to the opener (a11y-15).
- Delete `InlineCitation`, `CitationTooltip`, `SourcesFooter` (and their barrel exports in `citations/index.ts:8` and `lib/index.ts:21`), and the display maps in `lib/citations`. Keep only the `byToolCallId` lookup.

**Quoting the owner's own words.** For the NS: the model writes a blockquote whose last paragraph is only a ref link.
- The renderer draws the quote in full `--color-foreground` ink with a 2px `--color-border` rule.
- Padding-left is `calc(var(--prose-indent) - 2px)`.
- A 13px muted source line sits 4px below, for example "Nick · Messages · Sat, Jun 14, 2025, 11:52 PM", or "You · Messages · …".
- The box checks that the quoted text appears in the cited record, with whitespace normalized and "…" allowed for cuts. A quote that fails the check renders as an ordinary paragraph and is logged.
- Prompt slot: "Quote their words exactly as their records hold them, mark cuts with …, and never quote the narrative identity document." The last part is already a rule (`prompt.rs:46`).
- This is later work (slice 10).

**Message actions** (transcript-9, working-27).
- **Row.** margin-top 8px, gap 4px. `IconButton md` (28px with a 16px glyph), subtle ink, hover foreground on `--hover-bg`.
- **Actions.**
  1. **Copy.** Body parts only, from `splitTurn(...).bodyFromIndex`, joined with blank lines, with `[Name](/entity/id)` rewritten to "Name". On failure, a toast: "Couldn't copy. Select the text instead."
  2. **Try another answer.** Last reply only.
  3. **More (⋯).** Copy as Markdown, Save as a page, and Share… on the phone (`navigator.share`). Save as a page creates a page from the body verbatim, keeps mentions as mention nodes, titles it with the chat title, ends with a line linking back to the chat, and opens it beside the chat. It makes no model call.
- **Gating.** Each reply decides for itself with `!(isStreaming && isLastMessage)`. Today `chat.status === 'ready'` hides every row (`ChatView.svelte:2277`). The last finished reply shows its row at rest. Older replies show it on hover or focus-within, and always under `hover: none`.
- **Phone.** A 44px `::after` hit area per button.
- **Announcements.** "Copied" and "Saved as a page" go to the announcer.
- **Versions** (later, slice 10). After Try another answer, a "‹ 2 of 2 ›" control at 12/16 tabular, subtle ink, with arrows in 24px boxes (28px coarse). The box must keep earlier answers so versions survive a reload.

**The user message.**

| Property | Today | Proposed |
|---|---|---|
| Ground | `--color-surface-elevated` + 1px `--color-border` | `--prose-ground` with no border (Q15); the border returns under `prefers-contrast: more` |
| Radius | 1.5rem, off the scale | `--radius-2xl` (20px) |
| Padding, width | 10px 16px, max 80% | same; 88% in containers under 480px |
| Text | pre-wrap; only mentions become refs (`UserMessage.svelte:63-67`) | Marks the composer drew render through `readMessage()` (`message.ts:72`) as static `prose prose--compact`. `# ` and `- [ ] ` stay literal (Q41; this risks bringing back the VIR-333 misrender) |
| Long messages | Clip at 8 lines; arrow "Show more" in accent on the same ground | Never fold the message the current reply answers. Fold older ones past 12 lines (Q42) with `mask-image: linear-gradient(#000 calc(100% - 32px), transparent)` and a 13px muted TextAction "Show all" / "Show less" with `aria-expanded`. Re-measure with a ResizeObserver |
| Pasted text | download pill | Inset block (10.3) |
| Time | none | On hover (desktop) or long-press (phone): "9:41 AM", 12/16 tabular subtle, 12px left of the bubble on its last line |
| Actions | none | Same reveal: Copy for every user message, Edit for the last, as 28px buttons left of the bubble |

### 10.10 Figures

**Keep.**
- Data enters a figure only through SQL the box runs.
- Refusals that name the fix.
- Local time handling.
- The zero baseline for bars.
- The readout line.
- Tabular figures.
- The source line.
- Tiles from the box.

**Chart types.**

| Mark | Use for | Columns | Max rows | Notes |
|---|---|---|---|---|
| `bar` | sums and counts per bucket; categories | x, 1–3 series | 120 | zero baseline; `stack: true` for up to 3 parts |
| `line` | trends of measurements | x (time), 1–3 series | 400 | its own range, padded |
| `range` (later) | sleep window, daily min–max HR | x, `low`, `high` | 400 | low and high as numbers or timestamps (clock axis) |
| `point` (later) | pace per run; two-variable questions | x, y, optional `label` | 400 | no fitted line; the model states the count |
| `numbers` | 1–4 headline figures | one row | 1 | optional comparison line |
| `table` | exact rows | ≤8 columns | 50 | `format` per column |
| `timeline` | spans in a day or week | start, end, label | 199 | — |
| `map` | places, routes | lat/lon, optional `weight`, or `route` | 199 | — |

- **Not allowed:** pie and donut, area, dual y-axes (refused when two series have different formats), gauges and rings for health values, radar, and scores in colored bands.
- **Deferred:** the calendar heatmap (Q37). It resembles a streak grid.

**Schema additions to `show`** (B16). The model declares; the box validates and computes. The model never types a value.

```json
{
  "kind": "chart", "mark": "line", "title": "Time asleep by week",
  "sql": "SELECT week AS \"Week\", asleep AS \"Asleep\", nights AS n FROM sleep_nights_by_week WHERE week >= {start} AND week < {end}",
  "window": { "start": "2026-07-27", "end": "2026-10-19" },
  "bucket": "week", "fill": "gap",
  "format": { "Asleep": "duration_minutes" },
  "compare": "usual",
  "emphasis": ["2026-10-12"]
}
```

- **`format`** per column: `duration_minutes`, `duration_seconds`, `clock`, `currency_cents` (reads a `currency` column; refused when rows mix currencies), `count`, `percent`, `ratio`, `distance_meters`, `distance_km`, `pace_seconds_per_km`, `bpm`, `ms`, `kcal`, `hour_of_day`, `weekday`. Tables and numbers use it too.
- **`bucket`** (`hour`, `day`, `week`, `month`, `year`): x values must sit on bucket starts in the owner's zone, with Monday-start weeks from `date_trunc('week')`. Otherwise the call is refused: "use date_trunc('week', …) in the owner's zone".
- **`window`** with `{start}` and `{end}` placeholders, end exclusive. The box fills missing buckets with null rows and marks a bucket containing now as `open`. Whether `sql_query` can bind parameters today is unverified.
- **`fill`**: `"gap"` (default, for measurements) or `"zero"` (for sums of events).
- **`n`**: a reserved count column that feeds the source line and the readout.
- **`compare`**: `previous`, `year` or `usual`. Requires the placeholders.
- **`emphasis`**: x values the reply's sentence is about. The box checks that each exists.
- **`replaces`**: the ref of an earlier figure this one refines (section 11, #5).
- **Map**: `weight` (radius) and `route` (GeoJSON from `data_health_workout.route_geometry`).
- **New refusals**: mixed units, mixed currencies, dual axes, misaligned buckets, compare without placeholders.
- **Money.** `data_financial_transaction.amount` is a bigint in cents (`0001_initial.sql:1275`). The comment at `sql_query.rs:308-309` calls it `amount_cents`. Fix the comment, or rename the column with the full `columns.md` sweep (B25).
- **Sleep.** One sleep-night view (merged intervals attributed to the evening date) used by both `show` and the circumstances block (B17; Q18). Today a SUM over overlapping watch and phone records can double count.

**Sizes.**

| Part | ≥480px container | <480px |
|---|---|---|
| Figure margin | 24px above and below | same |
| Title | serif 18/24, 400, margin-bottom 4px | same |
| Readout row | 13px tabular, min-height 20px, margin-bottom 6px | same |
| bar/line SVG | 200 (plot 170) | 184 (plot 150) |
| Padding | top 8, right 8, bottom 22, left = widest y label × 5.8px + 8 (28–52) | bottom 26 on coarse pointers |
| Ticks | mono 9.5px in `--chart-tick` | 11px under `(pointer: coarse)` |
| x label spacing | ≥56px at 9.5px; ≥64px at 11px | — |
| Bars | 64% of the band, max 36px, min 2px; grouped 2px apart; radius `min(3px, width/4)` at the data end | — |
| Lines | series 1 at 2px; series 2–3 at 1.5px; round joins | — |
| Hover dot | r 4 with a 2px knockout | r 5 |
| Source line | 12px muted, margin-top 8px | wraps |
| Numbers | figure serif 36/40; unit letters serif 18px muted with a 2px gap; label 13px; comparison 13px muted; gap 16px × 40px | two per row |
| Map | 260px, `--radius-xl`; point r = 4 + 8·√(w/wmax) | 240px |

**Axis labels.** Choose by bucket, or by span when no bucket is declared. Fixes reply-ui-3. Examples use 2026 dates and Monday-start weeks.

| Bucket or span | Axis label | Readout |
|---|---|---|
| hour, or span under 36h | "9 AM" | "Tue, Oct 6, 9:00 AM" |
| day, span under 14 days | "Tue 6" | "Tue, Oct 6" |
| day, longer | "Oct 6" | "Tue, Oct 6" |
| week | "Oct 5" | "Week of Oct 5" |
| month | "Oct"; "Oct 2026" on the first tick and on January | "October 2026" |
| year | "2026" | "2026" |
| sleep night | "Tue 6" | "Tue night, Oct 6" |

- On a time axis, place labels at round time steps (1h, 3h, 6h, 1d, 1w, 1mo), by time and at least 56px apart.
- **y domain.** Bars include zero. Lines are padded so the y span is at least 10% of the mean. Durations tick in whole hours from a 3h span, and in "6h 30m" steps below that.
- **Gaps and zeros.**
  - A null bucket draws no mark, a 6px × 1px tick in `--chart-axis` at the baseline, and the readout "No record".
  - A bucket before the source's coverage start reads "Not recorded yet".
  - A zero draws a 2px stub in the series ink.
  - A line breaks at any null bucket. A point isolated between gaps draws as a 2.5px dot (reply-ui-7).
- **Open bucket.** The bar or last segment draws at 45% ink, with "so far" in the label. Readout: "This week so far · 3 of 7 nights".
- **Now.** A 1px `--chart-accent` line at the present.
- **Legends.** None inside the plot. At rest the readout carries the legend, with swatches that repeat each series' dash or hatch.

**"Your usual" band and comparisons** (Q18).
- `compare: "usual"` asks the box for the 25th to 75th percentile of the same SQL over the period before the window: 90 days for day buckets, 26 weeks for week buckets, 12 months for month buckets.
- With fewer than 14 earlier buckets, the box draws no band and returns a note ("no band: only 9 nights before the window").
- The band is a `--chart-band` rect behind the gridlines. Legend "Usual (Apr–Jul)". The readout adds "usual 7h 5m–7h 40m".
- The band never recolors a mark or flags a point. Whether anything is notable stays in the model's sentence.
- `previous` and `year` on a line draw the comparison at 1.5px in `--chart-ink-3` with a 2-3 dash. On bars they draw a 1.5px cap in `--chart-ink-2`, 4px wider than the bar.
- On numbers, a 13px muted line: "$264 in September". No arrows, and no red or green.

**Color tokens** (Q17). Defined once in `:root` and derived from each theme, so dark mode follows.

```css
--chart-ink-1: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-foreground) 86%, var(--color-page));
--chart-ink-2: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-foreground) 66%, var(--color-page));
--chart-ink-3: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-foreground) 50%, var(--color-page));
--chart-accent: var(--color-primary);
--chart-grid:  color-mix(in srgb, var(--wash-ink) 8%, var(--color-page));
--chart-axis:  color-mix(in srgb, var(--wash-ink) 28%, var(--color-page));
--chart-band:  color-mix(in srgb, var(--wash-ink) 6%, transparent);
--chart-tick:  var(--color-foreground-muted);
--chart-knockout: var(--color-page);
```

- Series 1 is `--chart-ink-1` solid. Series 2 is `--chart-ink-2` solid. Series 3 is `--chart-ink-3` with a 4-3 dash on lines and a 45° hatch on bars (4px pitch, 1px stroke). Pattern ids come from `$props.id()`. This fixes reply-ui-6, where bar series 2 and 3 are the same gray.
- **If Q17 keeps the ink ramp**, the accent marks only the hovered or selected mark, `emphasis` marks, the now line and pressable text. At rest the chart is ink.
- **If Q17 keeps today's primary-blue series 1**, emphasis draws the named mark at full ink and the others at 0.45 opacity.
- Knockouts (hover dots, map points) stroke with `--chart-knockout`. Today they use `--color-background` (`ShowChart.svelte:312`; `ShowMap.svelte:88`), while the chat sits on `--color-page`.
- Check contrast against every theme with a script: at least 3:1 for marks.

**Interaction** (mobile-2, a11y-7, reply-ui-14).
- **Pointer.** The nearest x across the full plot height. A 1px crosshair in `--chart-axis`. The hovered mark turns accent over 80ms and back over 200ms. Other marks are not dimmed.
- **Touch.** Pointerdown selects and pins the readout. A horizontal drag scrubs. `touch-action: pan-y` stays. A tap outside or on the same mark clears. A first tap never navigates.
- **Keyboard.** The plot group gets `tabindex=0; role="group"; aria-roledescription="chart"`. ←/→ step, Home and End jump, Enter opens a linked day, Escape clears. Focus ring 2px `--color-border-focus` at 2px offset.
- **Readout actions** while a mark is selected: "Open Wed, Oct 14" (`/day/day_2026-10-14`) and "Ask about this", which fills the composer with "What happened on Wed, Oct 14?" unsent. Both are 13px TextActions.
- **Screen readers.** The readout is announced only after a keyboard step; today it is `aria-live` on every pointer move. The label is generated from the rows, for example "Line chart, Asleep by week, 12 weeks from Jul 27 to Oct 12, lowest 6h 21m week of Sep 7, highest 7h 31m week of Aug 3." A visually hidden `<table>` of the rows follows every chart.
- **Timeline.** The svg becomes `role="group"`. Every item is focusable with a roving tabindex and handles Enter and Space (a11y-8).
- **Map.** Drop `role=img` and add a 13px list of places after the map for keyboard users.

**Source line.** `From {n or rows} {unit noun}, {window} · {where}`, followed by "See the rows" and "Copy…" (a menu: CSV, Add to a page, and, if Q9 allows, Image and Image without values).
- Example: "From 84 nights, Jul 27–Oct 18 · ran on your server".
- Numbers: "From 31 transactions · Oct 1–21 so far".
- When the rows went to a cloud model: "· rows sent to {slot display name}" (reply-ui-18; 10.11).
- CSV headers carry units, for example "Asleep (minutes)" and "Amount (USD)".

**Placeholder and motion.**
- The placeholder takes each kind's real height: chart 226, map 286, one-lane timeline 68, numbers 64, table 28 + 10×33.
- It has four `--chart-grid` lines and an opacity pulse from 0.5 to 1 over 1.6s, on `--prose-ground`. Today the gradient sweep is invisible in Pemberley (reply-ui-5, reply-ui-8).
- Arrival: a left-to-right `clip-path` reveal over 360ms on `--ease-premium`, only when this client watched the call resolve. Opacity only under reduced motion.
- Nothing counts up.

**Maps.**
- Clamp `maxZoom` to 7 when no point falls inside `atlasSquares()` (`atlas.ts:166-174`), with a 12px muted line: "Street detail for this area is not on your server."
- One marker spec everywhere: r 5, `--chart-accent` fill, 2px `--chart-knockout` stroke.
- Routes: 3px `--chart-accent` with a 2px knockout casing.
- The no-tiles fallback gets a 1px `--color-border` frame and "Your server has no map files for this area yet."
- Readout copy: "12 places" (reply-ui-23).

**Timeline.**
- Ticks snap to 15m, 30m, 1h, 3h, 6h, 1d or 1w steps, at least 90px apart. Dates appear on the first tick and at each midnight once the span passes 20h.
- A ninth lane labeled "+N more" opens the rows.
- In-bar labels are drawn only at 4.5:1 or better; otherwise they sit to the right in muted 9.5px mono.
- Copy: "N events" (reply-ui-22).

**Tables.**
- Hide `id` and `ref` columns when every row carries a ref.
- Header buttons sort on the client, with `aria-sort` (reply-ui-24).

**Plots from code.**
- Draw each plot once, at the call's place (slice 0).
- A box mplstyle reads tokens from environment variables: transparent figure and axes, Avenir, 0.5px gridlines at 15% ink, `--chart-ink-1` first.
- The code tool's description says: "Draw charts with show; save to out/ only a figure show cannot draw" (reply-ui-19).

**Numbers in prose.** Add this block to the prompt after `<about_their_life>` (`prompt.rs:31-33`). One formatter should cover figures, readouts and the circumstances block.

```
<numbers>
- Write durations as 7h 4m, money as $12.40 or $1,240, counts grouped (9,840), times in their own clock (11:40 PM).
- Every figure names its window and its count: "over 12 weeks (84 nights)".
- A comparison names both windows and gives the earlier value: "6h 50m, against 7h 15m in the three months before".
- Round, and say "about" when you do. Never more precision than their records have.
- A link between two things says how often they went together and leaves the cause to them: "on 9 of the 12 nights after a late dinner".
- Link a computed figure to its query ref.
- For sums of events, missing days are zero. For measurements such as sleep and heart rate, missing days are unknown, and you say so.
</numbers>
```

### 10.11 Memory, rules and where work ran

**The memory receipt** (Rec). An unframed ReplyRecord under the reply body, above the message actions, 6px below the last block. It appears only on a turn where `update_memory` returned `saved: true`, and always shows, regardless of any verbosity setting. Today the fold says only "Noted something to remember" (`toolPresentation.ts:237-241`), although the note's text is in the call's `input.content` and its id in `output.note_id`.
- **Copy by operation.** Notes in curly quotes. No icon.
  - add: `Remembered “You started walking before work in March.” · Change · Forget`
  - revise: `Updated a note: “…” · Change · Undo`
  - retire: `Forgot a note: “…” · Undo`
- **Type.** 12/16 muted. The quoted note is in foreground, clamped to 1 line on desktop and 2 under a coarse pointer. Clicking the quote expands it. Verbs are 12px TextActions in primary, separated by " · " in subtle. Each verb has a 24px hit area (44px coarse).
- **Change** opens a one-line field: 14px, up to 500 characters, Enter saves, Esc cancels. It saves through `edit_memory`, so the note becomes the owner's and the box refuses to let the model rewrite it.
- **Forget** retires the note. The line becomes "Forgot that note. · Undo" for 6000ms (the `UNDO_MS` in `RulesSettings.svelte:37`). Undo needs an un-retire endpoint (B15).
- **Several writes** in one turn: the first, then "+2 more", which opens the fold.
- **Announcement:** "Saved to memory" or "Forgot a note".
- **⋯ menu:** "Don't bring this up again". It writes an `avoid` rule through the rules append endpoint (B22) and retires the note. Things that must not be raised are kept in one store.
- **Temporary chat:** if `update_memory` runs there (not checked), the line adds "This note stays after the chat ends."
- **Backend.**
  - `source_chat_id` and `source_message_id` on `app_assistant_memories` (B15; migration 0013 has no source columns). Settings then shows a 12px muted link "From a chat on Oct 14".
  - Add to the tool description at `crates/virtues-registry/src/tools.rs:601`: "Only note what they told you in this conversation, never what you inferred from their records."
  - Escape `<` and `>` in note bodies before they enter the prompt (`chat.rs:1376`; knows-21).

**The rule card** (Rec; Q16). Needs `propose_rule {kind, rule}` and an append endpoint (B22). `saveNarrativeRules` replaces the whole set, so two writers would race.
- The tool is called only on a clear statement, such as "don't bring up drinking unless I do".
- **Pending** (ReplyCard). Caption 13px muted: "Keep this as a rule?" Rule text 15px foreground, using the owner's words from this turn: "Never raise drinking unless you do." or, for `defend`, "Help you hold to: no phone after 10 PM." Buttons: "Keep it" (primary) and "Not a rule" (quiet).
- **Settled.** The card becomes a record without the rule text: "Kept a rule · Settings". A declined card becomes "Not kept as a rule".
- **On reload** the card always renders settled.
- **Under "Keep off shared screens"** (Q27) the settled line reads "Kept a rule" with no link.
- No receipt elsewhere in the transcript names an avoided topic.

**The opening and titles** (Rec, DS; slice 0, B19).
- **Opening.** Reads `avoid` rules (10.5). Has a provenance line and an off switch.
- **Titles.**
  - Pass the `avoid` rules to the title prompt (`chats.rs:956-1060`) and ask for a neutral title when the chat touches one.
  - A chat-menu item "Keep this title private" sets `title_source` to the hand-set state and lists the chat as "Private chat" in muted ink, with no glyph, in the sidebar, the drawer, tabs and search.
  - Local chats skip generation and take their title from the first user line, cut to 6 words on the box (`ChatView.svelte:1688-1690`).
- The context view lists the title call and the opening call under "Background calls for this chat". Their spend buckets `chat_title` and `chat_opening` are already recorded. That the opening's `chat_id` is null is inferred.

**"Where this ran"** (DS). The last block of the opened fold, below the timeline. The narrative seeker never sees it unless they open "Worked for".
- **Title.** "Where this ran", 12px Avenir 500 in muted ink, 10px above the rows.
- **Rows.** A grid with columns 7rem and 1fr, gap 4px 12px, 13/20. Labels muted, values foreground, numbers tabular.

| Label | Value |
|---|---|
| Your server | Read messages (14 rows) and calendar (3 rows). Drew 1 figure. |
| {provider display name} | 3 model steps · 18.2K tokens in, 640 out · zero retention |
| Virtues | Passed the model calls through api.virtues.com (wallet route). For BYO the label is the endpoint host and the value is "Your own endpoint. Its terms apply." |
| The web | 1 search: “trailhead parking rules” · through Virtues |
| Your Mac | Browsed example.com (6 steps), or Sent a message to Nick |

- **Retention** from `zdr` (tri-state): `all` → "zero retention"; `none` → "May keep what it was sent"; `some` or unknown → nothing.
- **Closing sentence** for every cloud turn, 12/16 muted: "What was read on your server went to {provider} with your question."
- **Local turns** have one row: "Your server: Answered on the NPU · 212 tokens · 9.4 a second · first word in 1.8s". `LocalStatsLine` becomes this row.
- **Data.**
  - Provider and model display names come from metadata and the catalog, never literals.
  - Read nouns come from `runs_on` on ToolConfig (B6).
  - Steps, tokens and cost come from finish-step metadata, persisted on the row (B10).
  - `message_id` on `app_ai_calls` lets a reload rebuild the block from the log (B14).
  - Web search spend joins the turn (B20).

**The opt-in line under each reply.**
- A per-device setting, "Show where each reply ran", in Settings › Assistant and the chat ⋮ menu. Stored in localStorage inside try/catch. Off by default.
- With it on, one line sits under each finished reply beside the message actions: "Your server, then {provider} · zero retention · 18.2K in · $0.012". Local: "Stayed on your server · on the NPU". BYO: "Your server, then {endpoint host} · cost on your key".
- 12/16 in `--color-foreground-muted`, because subtle ink fails AA in several themes until slice 1. Clicking a segment opens the context view at that turn.
- **Shown whether or not the setting is on:**
  - A turn that used a model that may keep requests: "Sent to a model that keeps requests · {model display name}".
  - A turn whose route differs from the previous turn: "This reply went through Virtues; earlier ones used your key."

**The status-line location tail** is specified in 10.2. It is on by default only in Local chats, otherwise with the setting.

**The context view** (`ContextViewPanel.svelte`).
- **Order, top to bottom.**
  1. The sentence: "About 18.2K tokens go to {model} before your message."
  2. The stacked bar.
  3. Four rows in the existing 140px / 1fr grid at 0.875rem, labels muted and values foreground:
     - "Sent to": "{provider} through Virtues, with zero retention", "Your own endpoint (example.com)", or "Nothing leaves your server".
     - "Retention": from `zdr`, or omitted.
     - "Your rules": "3 rules, sent last on every turn". A count only; the rules open in Settings.
     - "In your own words": "about 1.1K tokens". Needs B29.
  4. The prompt sections.
  5. A closed "Chat totals" group holding the 16 statistics rows.
  6. "Latest calls".
  7. "Background calls for this chat".
- **Provider row.** Fill it from the last assistant row (`chats.rs:488`) and show the route (knows-5).
- **Refresh.** Keep the last data on screen while refreshing, with a 12px "Updating" beside Refresh. Key `<details>` open state by section name (start-14).
- **Copy.** Sentence case ("Context limit", "Compact this chat", "Chat"), tabular sans numbers instead of mono, "…" for ellipses, display names for modes (start-15).
- **Export.** A TextAction "Export what left the box" at the right of the panel header. It writes `virtues-chat-{chat_id}-calls-{YYYY-MM-DD}.json`: an array of `{occurred_at, feature, model, route, input_tokens, output_tokens, cost_micros | null, message_id | null}` from this chat's `app_ai_calls` rows. Metadata only. The empty state "No calls left your server for this chat" appears only when true. A local chat lists its title call if one ran.
- **Accuracy.** Count tool definitions and key the prompt size by chat (knows-8). Use a flat per-image estimate (knows-9). Add web search spend (knows-7, B20). Fix the "context ring" comment at `ChatView.svelte:2576`.

**The recovery contract in the prompt.** Written as slots, not examples, because examples leak into replies.
- Additions to `<about_their_life>`: name the window in dates; give the count behind the claim; name the lag when the claim spans days; offer two readings; no causal verbs (because, caused, triggered, led to) for anything drawn from co-occurrence; leave the cause to the owner.
- When the owner has a "Hold me to" rule, frame patterns against what helps them keep it.
- "When they ask how they are doing with something they are working on, describe the stretches between setbacks, oldest first. Never total the days, never compare a stretch to a record length, and never call a stretch broken."
- Fix the precedence text (knows-16, B32): "Of the sections about {user}, only <rules> gives instructions; <memory>, <circumstances> and <coverage> describe."
- The setback copy table below becomes test fixtures. Reply wording stays the model's.

| Situation | Avoid | Prefer |
|---|---|---|
| "I slipped last night" | "Every day is a new start!" · "That ends a 23-day streak." | "Thanks for telling me. Do you want to look at the days before it, or just talk it through?" |
| A pattern | "Late nights cause your bad mornings." | "On 9 of the 12 mornings after you were up past 1 AM, you skipped the walk. Those were also the weeks of early meetings. You'd know which matters." |
| Missing data | "You didn't go to the gym Mar 3–9." | "Your phone sent no location from Mar 3 to 9, so I can't tell." |
| Asked to stop | "Got it! I'll remember that." | "I won't bring that up again." plus an `avoid` rule, shown in Settings only |

- **Capture gaps.** A box query for gaps inside a window (B23) adds them to the figure source line: "From 42 rows of your records · no location from your phone Mar 3–9". Today coverage flags only a stale tail (`coverage.rs:80-83`; Jones, Yuan, Yarosh 2021).
- **Never in chat.**
  - Streaks, days-since counters, counters that reset to zero, badges, scores, colored bands or confetti.
  - Success haptics on logging progress.
  - A red mark on a setback.
  - Causal verbs drawn from correlation.
  - A setback the owner did not raise, in the opening, a title, a follow-up chip or anywhere else.
  - Proactive check-ins.
  - Any receipt that names an avoided topic.

### 10.12 Mobile

**Record sheet** (mobile-1). On the phone, anything opened from a reply opens as a sheet over the chat: table rows, map points, timeline items, @refs, created and edited pages, message recipients, and the rows view.
- **Size and shape.** Height `calc(100% - 12px - env(safe-area-inset-top))`, 16px radius on the top corners.
- **Grab handle.** 36×5px, 2.5px radius, `--color-border-strong`, 8px from the top.
- **Backdrop.** `color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-foreground) 20%, transparent)`.
- **Motion.** Opens, and drags down to dismiss, on the drawer's curve: 0.3s `cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1)`. Reduced motion: 0.12s opacity.
- **Header.** 48px: title 15/20 at 500 with ellipsis, a 44px close button at left, and "Open" as a 15px primary TextAction at right with a 44px hit area. Open navigates for real.
- The chat stays mounted underneath, so the reading position is kept.
- Correct the comment at `window-shell.svelte.ts:113-117` that calls history "the only back button the phone has".

**Composer on the phone.**
- **Dock padding-bottom:** `calc(0.5rem + max(env(safe-area-inset-bottom) - var(--keyboard-inset, 0px), 0px))`. Below the pill: 6px row margin + 24px row + that padding. That is 72px at rest with a 34px home indicator, and 38px with the keyboard up.
- **Width.** The gutter stays 1.5rem, so the pill is 327px at 375px.
- **Trailing cluster.** Send only. During a turn with a draft: Stop plus Send (68px).
- **Editor cap:** `min(200px, 30% of visual viewport height)`.
- **Enter** by input modality (10.3).
- **No autofocus on a new chat** (mobile-4).
- **Controls row.** 24px tall, 12px text, one 44px `::after` tap area. A tap opens the controls sheet.
  - Bottom sheet with a 16px top radius and the same grab handle, sitting above the keyboard. Max height `min(70dvh, 100dvh - var(--keyboard-inset) - env(safe-area-inset-top) - 24px)`. Drawer curve.
  - **Model:** a 44px row, "Model" left and "Auto ›" right, both 15px. Tapping slides in the model list.
  - **Depth:** a segmented control 36px tall inside a 44px row. The track is `--hover-bg` with a 10px radius. The selected segment is `--color-surface` with a 0 1px 2px shadow at 8% foreground. Labels 13px, with a 12px muted description beneath that tracks the selection.
  - **Access:** a segmented control, "Ask first | Full access". Choosing Full access reveals the confirm text and a full-width 44px "Turn on" button. Nothing changes until it is pressed.
- **The + menu** holds Add files and Take a photo. The modes leave it (mobile-18).
- **@ and / pickers** dock to the pill's top edge at full column width. Max height is the visible height minus the composer minus 16px. Rows are 48px (16px title, 13px meta), with no preview.
- **Transcript reserve.** `padding-bottom: calc(var(--composer-height) + 1rem)` under `(max-width: 768px), (pointer: coarse)` (mobile-12).

**Reading.**
- Charts: tap to select (10.10), 11px ticks, a 44×44px map hit box.
- Choices: stacked full-width rows below 480px (10.7).
- Approvals: stacked (10.8).
- Every control in a reply gets a 44px `::after` hit area: message actions, remove buttons, the task check, the thinking header, record lines, citation links and version arrows (mobile-6).
- **Enlarging** (mobile-3):
  - Plots, generated images and message images open in MediaLightbox, which gains pinch zoom (scale 1–4) and double-tap to 2× at the tap point. Below 1× it springs back with a 300ms transform on `--ease-premium`.
  - Chat follows the S/M/L reading size (10.6).
  - The shell reads `UIApplication.preferredContentSizeCategory` and injects a factor that multiplies `--md-body-size`, capped at 1.5. The values below are from memory of Apple's Body text style (17pt at Large) and must be checked.

    | Category | Body pt | Factor |
    |---|---|---|
    | xSmall | 14 | 0.82 |
    | Small | 15 | 0.88 |
    | Medium | 16 | 0.94 |
    | Large (default) | 17 | 1.00 |
    | xLarge | 19 | 1.12 |
    | xxLarge | 21 | 1.24 |
    | xxxLarge | 23 | 1.35 |
    | AX1 and above | 28+ | 1.50 (cap) |

  - Whole-page pinch zoom stays off until the owner decides (Q29; `agents/plan/mobile-ux-plan.md:38-41`).
- **Long-press on a reply body** opens a menu of 44px rows anchored to the press point: Copy, Select text, Share… (`navigator.share` with the text and chat link), Ask about this (quotes the first sentence into the composer) (mobile-14).
- **Hover rules** in chat move under `@media (hover: hover)`. `:active` gives touch feedback: instant on press, 0.25s ease-out on release (mobile-20).

**Shell.**
- **Title in the bar** (mobile-8). The chat title in the title slot: 15/20 Avenir at 500, one line, ellipsis, max-width `calc(100% - 120px)`. A project shows as a 12px muted second line with its 12px glyph. Tapping the title opens the chat menu anchored bottom-center under the bar. Remove the floating ⋮ chip on the phone (`ChatView.svelte:1888-1899, 2579-2641`).
- **Chat menu.** "Pin tab" becomes "Pin to drawer" / "Unpin from drawer", backed by `pinsStore` (mobile-9). Add "Turns in this chat": one 44px row per user turn, with your first line at 15px and the answer's first words at 13px muted. Heading items from long answers are indented 16px under their turn. The context row shows its warning and error state as a colored dot through MenuItem's leading snippet (mobile-10).
- **Drawer** (mobile-13).
  - Menu gets `aria-expanded` and `aria-controls`.
  - On open, focus moves to the close button after the 0.3s settle. On close it returns to Menu.
  - Escape closes. While open the drawer is `role="dialog" aria-modal="true"`.
  - Long-press a row for pin, rename, move to project and delete, as 44px rows anchored below the row, flipping above near the bottom, through ContextMenuProvider (mobile-15).
- **Offline strip.** Overlays the view at `position: absolute; top: calc(48px + 6px)`, z-index 20 (above the view, below `--z-popover` at 60), with a 200ms opacity fade. It no longer pushes the view down. Messages sent while unreachable are held as queued chips (mobile-19).
- **Landscape on notched iPhones** (Q20). Add `padding-left: env(safe-area-inset-left); padding-right: env(safe-area-inset-right)` to `main.is-mobile`, or lock the iPhone to portrait in `Info.plist` until landscape is designed (mobile-16).
- **Copy** (mobile-21): "Leaving this chat ends it." · "Temporary chat. It won't be saved." · "This phone couldn't read that file. Try picking it again." · "Can't reach your server" everywhere.
- **Stale comments** (mobile-22): the bottom-tab comments in `(app)/+layout.svelte:440, 619-660`, `lib.rs:609-610`, `ChatInput.svelte:95-96`, `reachability.svelte.ts:16-17`, and `ContextMenuItem.svelte:15-23`.
- **iPad.** Keeps the phone shell for now (mobile-25). At 1024px and wider, plan a 320px persistent sidebar and 420px side panels in place of sheets.

**Mac.**
- The browser handoff and the first `browser_open` reach chat as records (10.7).
- ⌘W closes the active tab and the wiki moves to ⌘⇧W (Q19).
- Dictation listening state as in 10.3.

### 10.13 Long chats

**Send-to-top** (transcript-2).
- On send, tween the scroller with rAF over 320ms on `--ease-premium` until the user bubble sits 64px below the scroller's top. The end of the previous reply stays visible above it, which tells the reader where they are. On panes under 600px tall (most phones with the keyboard up) use 24px, because the peek costs too much height there.
- The in-flight turn gets a temporary `min-height: scroller.clientHeight − composer height − bubble height − offset`, removed once the reply is taller. The reply fills downward in view, and nothing moves under the reader.
- Any wheel, touchstart, pointerdown or keydown releases the hold (the input set from `holdReadingPosition.ts`). Jump-to-latest stays at 240px.
- This sits beside `holdReadingPosition`; it does not replace it.
- Reduced motion jumps instantly.

**Time in the transcript** (transcript-10).
- **Day divider** when consecutive turns fall on different local days. Centered, 12px Avenir in muted ink, 24px above and 16px below, no rule lines. Example: "Wednesday, October 21", with the year added when it differs from the current one. A same-day gap of 6h or more gets "Later · 6:40 PM".
- **Send times** beside bubbles on hover or long-press (10.9). The place (an opt-in "9:41 AM · at Elm St") is Q26 and never shows on shared screens.
- **Return line.** When a chat opens and its last message is 3 or more days old, a line at the end of the transcript reads "Last message 9 days ago" with a TextAction "Catch me up". It fills the composer with "What's happened since we last talked about this?" and does not send.

**The rail** (transcript-13, a11y-13, start-19).
- **Keyboard.** Roving tabindex: one tab stop; ↑/↓ move; Home and End jump; Enter goes. A focus outline on the mark: 2px `--color-border-focus`, offset 3px. Remove the `title=` that doubles the card. On activate, move focus to the target article with `preventScroll` after the scroll.
- **Card.** Gains a third line with the date, "Wed, Oct 21". Marks get an extra 6px gap between days.
- **Heading ticks.** A finished reply taller than twice the scroller height, with two or more `##`/`###` headings, adds level-2 items after its turn: 7px at rest, 17px active, 23px hovered (today's level-1 widths minus 3px). The card shows the heading at 13px and the section's first 120 characters at 12px subtle. Heading ids are `{messageId}-s{n}`.
- **Performance.** Items are memoized by message id and never computed for the streaming reply (transcript-17).
- **Narrow panes and phones.** Below 1000px, and on phones, the same items appear in the chat menu's "Turns in this chat".
- **⌥↑/⌥↓** work at every width (10.4).
- Delete `TableOfContents.svelte`.
- The rail reads element positions through `topOf` (`Rail.svelte:110`), so any long-chat performance work must keep messages in the DOM. Use `content-visibility`, not windowing.

**Transcript semantics** (a11y-3).
- Wrap the scroller's messages in `<section aria-label="Conversation" tabindex="0">`, so a keyboard user can focus it and scroll with arrow and Page keys.
- Each turn is an `<article tabindex="-1" aria-labelledby>` with an sr-only `<h2>` reading "You" or the assistant's name, plus the time where it is shown.
- Reply headings sit one level below (10.6).
- Add `scroll-padding-bottom: var(--composer-height)` to the scroller, so a focused control is never hidden under the composer (WCAG 2.4.11).

**Compaction** (start-7, start-16).
- Rewrite the summary template for a life: people and places mentioned; what they asked and what they were told; what they said about themselves, kept close to their words; plans and commitments; feelings named; open questions; progress and setbacks.
- The checkpoint becomes a ReplyRecord (10.7).
- The box sends `data-phase {phase:"compacting"}` before compaction (B4), so the status line says "Summarizing earlier messages". The context ring, if kept, warns at 70% with the hover text "Your assistant will summarize older messages at 85%."

**Performance** (start-20, transcript-17).
- Compute the exchange index once in a `$derived` Map. Today it is O(n²) at `ChatView.svelte:1927-1932`.
- Memoize the rail.
- `content-visibility: auto` on turns other than the streaming tail.

**Opening a long chat on WebKit** (transcript-11). After load, keep scrolling to the end for up to 1500ms with a ResizeObserver on `.messages-container`, releasing on any input. Store image dimensions with file parts and set width, height and aspect-ratio.

**Answer length.** Add a slot line to `<output_format>` (`prompt.rs:24`): "A question about a stretch of their life, a person, or who they were asks for detail; answer it in paragraphs."

---

## 11. Shareable features for the prosumer, with motion specs

Three rules filter this list.
1. A feature that runs on every send or every turn gets at most a 150ms opacity change or the 60ms press.
2. Nothing personal appears on a shared surface by default.
3. No frame of any animation shows a value that is not in the owner's records.

Timings use `--ease-premium` cubic-bezier(0.2,0,0,1), `--motion-state` 150ms, `--motion-enter` 180ms and `--motion-layout` 300ms (`themes.css:144-156`).

| # | Feature | Example post (invented) | Motion spec | Reduced motion | Needs | Slice |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| 1 | The ∴ deepens into a 5-cell and lands as one point | "the thinking mark in my self-hosted assistant is a 4-simplex rotating through w" | Depth from real passes (10.1); 300ms pose blend; precess `m3` held at 1; 960ms landing (Q1). Option-click the mark to copy a 2× PNG of the current pose, which holds no data | Still poses with a 150ms opacity crossfade | Step-driven depth | 2 |
| 2 | The status line says where the work runs | "it tells me, live, when a request leaves my house" | Shared-prefix label change: old tail fades over 120ms, new tail over 180ms from 60ms; location tail crossfades over 150ms | Opacity crossfade or instant swap | B6, B10; "Show where each reply ran" on outside Local chats | 6 |
| 3 | Send-to-top: the question pins near the top and the answer fills in below | "the answer writes downward into reserved space and nothing jumps" | rAF scroll over 320ms to 64px below the top (24px on short panes); temporary min-height; released by any input | Instant jump | None | 8 |
| 4 | A chart draws itself, and the bar the sentence names is the lit one | "asked how I slept since June and the week it mentioned lit up" | `clip-path` from `inset(0 100% 0 0)` over 360ms, only when this client watched the call land. Emphasis mark in the accent with other marks in ink (Q17 ink ramp), or the named mark at full ink and the others at 0.45 (Q17 blue series) | 150ms opacity | B16 (`emphasis`) | 5 |
| 5 | "Same, by week" redraws from the old chart | "said 'by week' and the daily bars slid together" | The new figure mounts at its reserved height and FLIPs from the old geometry: bar x and width over 300ms, heights on a critically damped spring of about 350ms with no bounce, y ticks crossfade over 150ms. The readout stays hidden until the bars settle. The old figure stays at 0.6 with "Redrawn below" | Opacity only | B16 (`replaces`) | 10 |
| 6 | Every figure links to its SQL | "every chart cites the query that made it, run on a mini PC in my closet" | The rows view opens beside the chat over 180ms; Shiki-highlighted SQL at 12/18 in a disclosure; caption "214 rows · ran on your server at 9:42 AM in 38 ms" | Instant | B3 (call duration) | 4 |
| 7 | Share a figure as an image | An image card footed "From 214 rows of my own records · queried on my own server" | Preview sheet scales 0.96→1 with opacity over 180ms. The card is 1200px at 2× on the theme ground: serif title 18px at 400, the figure, a 13px source line, and a 12px resting ∴ at bottom right. Maps excluded. Labels that match people start hidden, with a 12/16 muted line under the preview, "Hid 2 labels that name people · Show them". "Image without values" drops y ticks and readout numbers. Buttons: Copy image (Mac), Share… (iOS), Save image | Opacity fade | Share module; people lookup | 10, if Q9 allows |
| 8 | Steer a long turn without stopping it | "fixed its course mid-research without stopping it" | ⌥Enter sends a steer. When it lands at the next tool boundary, the chip FLIPs from the tray into the steps fold as a line ("You: no, the trip in May") over 300ms, its slot collapses over 150ms, and the label changes to "Changing course" | Instant | Injection at a step boundary in the agent loop (inferred feasible) | 10 |
| 9 | Edit the last question and keep both answers | — | ↑ in an empty composer refills it; the old bubble dims to 0.5; the tray shows "Editing your message · Cancel"; sending regenerates and keeps version 1. "‹ 1 of 2 ›" digits roll with translateY 0.6em→0 plus opacity over 200ms ease-out | Opacity only | The box keeps earlier answers | 10 |
| 10 | The opening line becomes turn one | — | FLIP from the hero to turn one over 400ms on `cubic-bezier(0.76,0,0.24,1)`, timed with the composer glide; crossfade from 280 to 400ms; the sky fades over 200ms | 150ms opacity | Threading change (Q31) | 7 |
| 11 | Hold ⌘ in a chat to see every key | — | Kbd badges on Stop (⌘.), the controls row (⌘⇧M) and the composer (/, ↑). Each badge: opacity 0→1 and translateY 2px→0 over 80ms, out over 200ms. Reuses `modifierHint`'s bare-modifier guard | Opacity only | Keyboard map | 3 |
| 12 | Local mode with a speedometer | "first word in 1.8s, 24 tokens a second, on the NPU, nothing left the box" | The stats row updates live in tabular digits (opacity over 350ms ease-out; transform on a spring with no bounce). A second line at 12/16 muted: "Nothing left your server." Model name from `/api/local-model` | Instant updates | B21; Q40 (new NPU work) | 6 |
| 13 | The context X-ray | — | In the context view, bar segments tween their widths over 300ms on refresh, and hovering a segment highlights its rows | Instant | — | 6 |
| 14 | The figure-eight easter egg | "type ∴ into an empty chat" | ThreeBody as today; its 300ms fade becomes 0 under reduced motion (`ThreeBody.svelte:282`) | Still plate (exists) | None | keep |

Removed because they fire tens of times a day:
- The top-bar pop-in on every chat open (260ms with overshoot, `ChatView.svelte:2646`).
- The send icon's 90° rotation with overshoot.
- The sky's endless twinkle.

The context ring in the controls row (10.4) is a separate item in slice 3, pending Q30.

**Backward compatibility.** The app can be newer or older than the server, so no request field is tightened. The server keeps accepting the old `agent_mode` field and maps it as in 10.4. B11 overrides the requested mode for a stored-local chat instead of refusing the turn. New metadata fields are optional on the client.

**Migrations.** B11, B14 and B15 each need a migration number claimed first with `make migration NAME=…`, following `.claude/rules/migrations.md`. 0060 is already claimed by `request_spend_limits` (a98786769). B2 needs no migration if parts stay JSON.

## 13. Open questions for the owner

1. **Landing length.** 960ms in place of 2000ms, judged after the mount fix lands?
2. **Shimmer.** Keep it at a fixed 125px/s, or drop it and leave the mark as the only working signal?
3. **"Worked for" on every reply.** Keep a one-line header on every reply for a stable layout, or collapse it over 300ms after landing and accept a 38px shift?
4. **Shift+Tab.** Remove mode cycling, at the cost of the Claude Code habit some prosumers have?
5. **Deep research.** Lasts one message, or sticks for the chat?
6. **Controls row.** Always visible under the pill, or reserved but empty until something differs from the default?
7. **Stop in ink.** Draw Stop in ink to set it apart from Send's primary?
8. **Novelty ink in the sky.** Keep the 0.9 ink for novelty z ≥ 1, as `attention-plan.md` argues, or drop it so the model's halo is the only emphasis?
9. **Share a figure as an image.** Build it (maps excluded, people's labels hidden by default, "Image without values" offered) or never? Do exported images carry a "Virtues" footer?
10. **Also lines.** Allow up to two more model-chosen lines under the opening sentence?
11. **Name links.** Names in replies in ink with a solid hairline, or in the accent as today? Does the day biography move to the solid line too?
12. **Serif emphasis.** `strong` as Avenir Medium beside Garamond, and `em` set roman in serif contexts?
13. **KaTeX italic variables.** The one sanctioned exception to "no italic serif", or forced upright?
14. **Open tables on pages.** Use them on pages too, with cell guides only while editing, or in chat only?
15. **Bubble border.** Remove it, keeping it under `prefers-contrast: more`?
16. **Rule proposals.** May the assistant propose a "Never bring up" or "Hold me to" rule from an ordinary chat, or only from the interview?
17. **Chart color.** An ink ramp, with the accent kept for selection, emphasis and now, or a primary-blue first series as today?
18. **The usual band.** Does a box-computed percentile band that the model asks for fit "significance is the model's judgment"? Is sleep attributed to the evening date or the wake date? Pace with faster at the top?
19. **⌘W.** In the Mac app, ⌘W closes the active tab and the wiki moves to ⌘⇧W?
20. **iPhone landscape.** Lock to portrait until landscape is designed, or support it now with side safe areas?
21. **Applet gates.** The 2026-10-06 note says to drop Allow for chat-authored applets. Should `setup_applet` and `edit_applet` leave `PERMISSION_REQUIRED` (`executor.rs:291-307`)?
22. **`/remember`.** Is a direct memory write with no model call acceptable, defaulting to the facts lane and editable afterwards?
23. **Legacy rooms.** Retire the interview and getting-started rooms from ChatView now that setup lives at `/setup`?
24. **Provenance wording.** Are "on your server", "sent to the web" and "Sent to {provider} through Virtues" the right words? Should exports and shared images name the provider, or only the box?
25. **The opening on local-preferring boxes.** Write it with the local model, or skip it?
26. **Place beside send times.** Should a place ever appear beside a send time, even as an opt-in?
27. **Shared screens.** Should "Keep off shared screens" exist per chat, enforced in the data sent to the kiosk?
28. **Persona order.** The brief or the persona note: which order should the next design round use?
29. **Pinch zoom.** Allow whole-page pinch zoom in the native shell (`mobile-ux-plan.md:38-41`)?
30. **Context meter.** A ring in the composer's controls row from 50%, or context only in the ⋮ menu?
31. **Threading the opening.** Thread it into the chat only when the owner chooses it, or automatically on any first send as today?
32. **"Needs you" on the new chat.** A "Needs you" line in the new-chat hero, given that "Needs you" was the removed Home's destination and design-grammar forbids rebuilding Home without addressing why it was removed?
33. **Depth.** Depth as a control, or Deep research as a skill per the recorded "skills, not modes" direction?
34. **Model picker.** A model picker with "All models…", or show the model only when it differs from the slot?
35. **Sky arrival.** Play the sky's arrival once per part of the day, then show it at rest?
36. **Answered choices and serif quotes.** Hide choices once they are answered? Serif quotes in replies only?
37. **Calendar heatmap.** Keep it deferred because it reads like a streak grid?
38. **Grounds on Pemberley.** Apply `--prose-ground` there, overriding its flat-white intent?
39. **"On this day".** Feed earlier years' events on this date to the opening, given anniversaries of setbacks and the rule against resurfacing struggles?
40. **NPU work.** The local speedometer, persisted local stats and voice transcription on the box are new NPU work, and the owner's notes say no new NPU work. Allow any of it?
41. **User echo.** Render the user's sent message through `readMessage`, accepting the VIR-333 risk the devil's advocate raised?
42. **User message fold.** Fold at 12 lines or 16? Never fold the message the current reply answers?
