## Appendix A. Motion in code

These are sketches. Field and variable names follow the audits' reading of the files. None of this has been run.

**A.1 The reduced-motion helper.**

```ts
// apps/web/src/lib/motion.ts (setup/motion.ts re-exports reducedMotion)
import { prefersReducedMotion } from 'svelte/motion';
export const reducedMotion = (): boolean => prefersReducedMotion.current;
export const dur = (ms: number): number => (reducedMotion() ? 0 : ms);

// One easing function for JS, matching --ease-premium cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0, 1)
function bezier(x1: number, y1: number, x2: number, y2: number) {
  const cx = 3 * x1, bx = 3 * (x2 - x1) - cx, ax = 1 - cx - bx;
  const cy = 3 * y1, by = 3 * (y2 - y1) - cy, ay = 1 - cy - by;
  const x = (t: number) => ((ax * t + bx) * t + cx) * t;
  const dx = (t: number) => (3 * ax * t + 2 * bx) * t + cx;
  const y = (t: number) => ((ay * t + by) * t + cy) * t;
  return (p: number) => {
    let t = p;
    for (let i = 0; i < 6; i++) { const d = dx(t); if (Math.abs(d) < 1e-6) break; t -= (x(t) - p) / d; }
    return y(Math.min(1, Math.max(0, t)));
  };
}
export const easePremium = bezier(0.2, 0, 0, 1);
```

**A.2 ThinkingMark.** This assumes a dot is `[cx, cy, r, opacity, z]`, matching the audit's "Dot[4]" for z.

```ts
export const LAND_MS = 960;
const LAND_CONV: Track = [[0, 0], [0.375, 1], [0.583, 1], [1, 0]];

// precess: the fourth vertex stays raised for the whole loop, so the wrap is invisible
precess: { duration: 4.2, linear: true, tilt: [[0, 56], [1, 56]], m3: [[0, 1], [1, 1]] /* spin unchanged */ },

// mount: start in the movement for the current depth
let running = untrack(() => movement);

// movement change
const BLEND_MS = 300;
const is3D = (m: Movement) => m === 'fold' || m === 'fourth' || m === 'descent';
function maybeSwitch(next: Movement, now: number) {
  if (next === running) return;
  const d = SCRIPTS[running].duration * 1000;
  const toBoundary = d - ((now - loopStart) % d);
  if (is3D(running) && is3D(next) && toBoundary <= 800) { pendingCut = next; return; }
  blendFrom = currentPose; blendStart = now; running = next; loopStart = now;
}
function blended(target: Pose, now: number): Pose {
  if (!blendFrom) return target;
  const t = Math.min(1, (now - blendStart) / BLEND_MS);
  if (t === 1) { blendFrom = null; return target; }
  const e = easePremium(t);
  return target.map((d, i) => {
    const s = blendFrom![i] ?? [d[0], d[1], 0, 0, d[4]];
    return d.map((v, k) => s[k] + (v - s[k]) * e) as Dot;
  });
}

// paint far dots first
let lastOrder = '';
function reorder(pose: Pose) {
  const order = pose.map((d, i) => [d[4], i] as const).sort((a, b) => a[0] - b[0]).map(([, i]) => i);
  const key = order.join();
  if (key !== lastOrder) { for (const i of order) svg.appendChild(circles[i]); lastOrder = key; }
}

// pause offscreen, resume from the same elapsed time
const io = new IntersectionObserver(([e]) => {
  if (e.isIntersecting) { resumeAt(elapsed); } else { cancelAnimationFrame(raf); }
});
io.observe(svg);
```

**A.3 The hanging mark.**

```css
.block-header { position: relative; }
.block-header .mark { position: absolute; left: -24px; top: 2px; width: 16px; height: 16px; }
@container (max-width: 480px) {
  .block-header .mark { position: static; margin-right: 8px; }
}
```

**A.4 Shimmer.**

```css
.label.shimmer {
  color: transparent;
  background-image: linear-gradient(90deg,
    var(--color-foreground-muted) 0 160px,
    var(--color-foreground) 200px,
    var(--color-foreground-muted) 240px 400px);
  background-size: 400px 100%;
  background-repeat: repeat-x;
  -webkit-background-clip: text; background-clip: text;
  animation: label-sweep 3.2s linear infinite;
}
.label.shimmer::selection { -webkit-text-fill-color: var(--color-foreground); }
@keyframes label-sweep { to { background-position-x: -400px; } }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .label.shimmer { animation: none; background: none; color: var(--color-foreground-muted); }
}
```

**A.5 Label hold and crossfade.**

```ts
let shown = $state('Thinking');
let shownAt = 0;
let timer: ReturnType<typeof setTimeout> | undefined;
$effect(() => {
  const next = thinkingLabel;
  const terminal = isTerminal(next);                 // "Writing", your-turn labels, finished text
  const wait = terminal ? 0 : Math.max(source === 'tool' ? 250 : 0, 700 - (performance.now() - shownAt));
  clearTimeout(timer);
  timer = setTimeout(() => { shown = next; shownAt = performance.now(); }, wait);
  return () => clearTimeout(timer);
});
```

```svelte
<span class="label-row">
  <span class="prefix">{sharedPrefix}</span>
  <span class="tail-stack">
    {#key tail}
      <span in:fade={{ duration: dur(180), delay: dur(60), easing: easePremium }} out:fade={{ duration: dur(120) }}>{tail}</span>
    {/key}
  </span>
</span>
<style>
  .label-row { display: flex; min-width: 0; }
  .tail-stack { display: grid; min-width: 0; }
  .tail-stack > :global(*) { grid-area: 1 / 1; overflow: hidden; text-overflow: ellipsis; white-space: nowrap; }
</style>
```

**A.6 Streamdown.**

```svelte
<Streamdown
  class="streamdown-content prose prose--reply"
  components={{ math: StreamdownMath }}
  animation={{ enabled: animate, type: 'fade', duration: 200,
               timingFunction: 'cubic-bezier(0.2, 0, 0, 1)', tokenize: 'word', animateOnMount: false }}
  {...rest} />
```

Turning off the block-level fade used the audit's selector, `.markdown :global([data-streamdown-paragraph][style*="sd-"])`. Check the attribute names against `svelte-streamdown/dist/Elements/Element.svelte` before use.

**A.7 Send button: press and icon swap.**

```css
.send { transition: background-color var(--motion-state) var(--ease-premium),
                    transform var(--motion-release) var(--ease-premium); }
.send:active { transform: scale(0.94); transition-duration: var(--motion-press); }
.icon-swap { display: grid; }
.icon-swap > .glyph { grid-area: 1 / 1; opacity: 0; transform: scale(0.85); transition: none; }
.icon-swap > .glyph.on { opacity: 1; transform: scale(1);
  transition: opacity 150ms var(--ease-premium), transform 150ms var(--ease-premium); }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .icon-swap > .glyph.on { transform: none; transition: opacity 120ms linear; }
  .send:active { transform: none; }
}
```

**A.8 ReplyCard settling and the ReplyRecord fold.**

```css
.reply-card { border: 1px solid var(--color-border); background: var(--color-surface);
  border-radius: var(--radius-xl); padding: 16px;
  transition: border-color var(--motion-layout) var(--ease-premium),
              background-color var(--motion-layout) var(--ease-premium); }
.reply-card.settling { border-color: transparent; background-color: transparent; }
```

```ts
// swap to the record when the fade ends; at once under reduced motion
function settle(el: HTMLElement, done: () => void) {
  if (reducedMotion()) return done();
  el.classList.add('settling');
  el.addEventListener('transitionend', done, { once: true });
}
```

```svelte
{#if open}
  <div class="fold"
       in:slide={{ duration: dur(180), easing: easePremium }}
       out:slide={{ duration: dur(150), easing: easePremium }}>…</div>
{/if}
```

**A.9 Figure reveal.** From-only keyframes, per design-grammar §7.

```css
@keyframes figure-reveal { from { clip-path: inset(0 100% 0 0); } }
@keyframes figure-fade { from { opacity: 0; } }
.figure.revealing .plot { animation: figure-reveal 360ms var(--ease-premium) both; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .figure.revealing .plot { animation: figure-fade 150ms linear both; }
}
```

**A.10 Sheets.**

```css
.sheet { transform: translateY(100%); transition: transform 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1); }
.sheet.open { transform: translateY(0); }
.sheet-backdrop { background: color-mix(in srgb, var(--color-foreground) 20%, transparent);
  opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.3s cubic-bezier(0.32, 0.72, 0, 1); }
.sheet-backdrop.open { opacity: 1; }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .sheet { transform: none; opacity: 0; transition: opacity 0.12s linear; }
  .sheet.open { opacity: 1; }
}
```

**A.11 Composer glide, transform only.**

```css
.composer-dock { transition: transform 0.4s cubic-bezier(0.76, 0, 0.24, 1); }
.composer-dock.is-empty { transform: translateY(calc(-50cqh + 50%)); }
@media (prefers-reduced-motion: reduce) {
  .composer-dock { transition: opacity 150ms linear; }
}
```

The `cqh` value assumes `.chat-area` is a size container. If it is not, compute the offset in JS once per resize.

## Appendix B. Standing decisions at risk

| Decision | Where the code or a proposal breaks it | Action |
|---|---|---|
| Never bold serif | LocalModelCard's `<h2>` title at weight 600 (start-23); the "Virtues" text-stroke (`NewChatSky.svelte:167`); h5 and h6 faux bold (type-2); the MobileDrawer wordmark's 0.2px text-stroke (navigation is out of scope; listed as a known violation) | Slices 1, 6, 7 |
| No italic serif | Synthesized italics in serif contexts (type-4); KaTeX variables | 10.6; Q13 |
| No uppercase tracked serif | The lifeline "now" label (start-24) | Slice 10, if the rooms stay |
| Significance is the model's judgment | The sky's novelty ink (Q8); the usual band (Q18) | Owner questions |
| Never rebuild Home | The widget sketch; a "Needs you" line (Q32) | Cut; Q32 |
| No hardcoded badge colors | Citation `:global(.text-*)` overrides (`InlineCitation.svelte:136-192`); `rgba` literals in cards | Slices 1, 4 |
| No hardcoded model ids | The LocalModelCard copy "Qwen3 0.6B" (`LocalModelCard.svelte:26`) | B21 |
| Applets auto-allow | Applet gate copy in 10.8 | Conditional on Q21 |
| No new NPU work | Section 11 #12; on-box voice transcription | Q40 |
| APNs sends nothing | No feature here promises a phone alert | Keep it that way |
| No streaks or games for recovery | The calendar heatmap stays deferred (Q37) | 10.11's never-in-chat list |
| Airlock ceremony reverted | Nothing here reintroduces it | — |
| iMessage reply UI cut | Nothing here reintroduces it | — |
| Never lead day summaries with step counts | The opening's rules stay in force | 10.5 |
| Never tighten a request field | B11's override replaces a refusal | 12.1 |
## Appendix C. Every audit finding

This appendix lists all audit findings with the second reader's result. "Partly" means the problem is real but a detail in the first reading was wrong; the correction is in the last column. Refuted findings are listed so nobody rebuilds on them. Section 5.1 repeats the high-severity ones with their slice.

Deep = Default/High, 50 steps, $10, 25 min, uncapped, Deep slot, dispatch_subagents allowed, the orchestrator guidance moved to a tail block like a skill (chat.rs:1433) so the cached head stays the same. ### Accessibility, keyboard and motion (chat UI)

| ID | Sev | Kind | Problem | Where | Recommendation | Check |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| a11y-1 | high | a11y | Shift+Tab in the composer cycles the chat mode, and two presses from Chat reach Sudo without any announcement | `apps/web/src/lib/document/composer.ts:403` | Give Shift+Tab back to focus traversal. Move mode cycling to a chord the registry can list, for example mod+. or mod+shift+m, formatted with shortcuts.format(). Keep Sudo out of any cycle: entering it should take an explicit menu pick, then a one-line confirm in the chip ('Sudo is on for this chat. Commands run without asking'), announced through an sr-only role=status next to the composer. Expose the active mode on the editable with aria-describedby pointing at the chip. | confirmed |
| bug-2 | high | bug | Approval cards stay live in old turns; Allow on a stale card acts on the newest turn, and Deny does nothing | `apps/web/src/lib/components/tabs/views/ChatView.svelte:2045` | Pass an `active` prop the way Show/ShowChoices do (ChatView.svelte:2149), true only for the newest finished turn. Render older cards resolved: 'Allowed' or 'Not allowed', read from the persisted grant (editAllowListStore) or from the turn that followed. Wire Deny to send a short refusal turn ('Not allowed. Don't run that.') so the model learns the answer, and persist it. | confirmed |
| a11y-3 | high | a11y | The transcript has no semantics: a screen reader cannot tell your messages from the assistant's or jump between them | `apps/web/src/lib/components/tabs/views/ChatView.svelte:1933` | Make the messages container role="log" with aria-label="Conversation" and aria-live="off", since the thinking block already announces. Wrap each turn in <article> with aria-labelledby pointing at an sr-only heading such as 'You said' or 'Your assistant replied, 3:14 PM'. Use h2 for the labels so the H key moves between turns. | ID | Sev | Kind | Problem | Where | Recommendation | Check |

| transcript-1 | high | brief-mismatch | Math is not rendered; raw TeX shows in a code span | `apps/web/src/lib/components/Markdown.svelte:128` | Import the component (`import StreamdownMath from 'svelte-streamdown/math'`) and pass `components={{ math: StreamdownMath }}`. Add `.prose .katex-display { margin-top: var(--prose-gap); overflow-x: auto; padding: 0.25em 0 }` and `.prose .katex { font-size: 1.05em }` to prose.css so pages can reuse it. Until then, mark Math as Open in the brief. | confirmed |
| transcript-2 | high | ux | Nothing follows the stream, and the answer starts at the bottom edge | `apps/web/src/lib/components/tabs/views/ChatView.svelte:1494` | Adopt send-to-top. On send, scroll so your bubble sits 24px below the scroller top. Give the in-flight turn a temporary min-height of (scroller.clientHeight - composer height - bubble height - 24px), and drop it once the reply is taller. The reply then fills downward in view, and nothing moves under a reader. Keep jump-to-latest at 240px. If a follow mode is preferred instead: follow only while within 48px of the end, release on the same INPUT_EVENTS holdReadingPosition uses (wheel, touchstart, pointerdown, keydown), and resume when jump-to-latest is pressed. | confirmed |

### While the assistant works: the ∴ thinking mark, the status line, the "Worked for Ns" steps fold, tool naming, browsing and research-worker display, stop and error endings, and what stays on screen after a turn

| ID | Sev | Kind | Problem | Where | Recommendation | Check |
|---|---|---|---|---|---|---|
| working-1 | high | bug | A plain reply drops its whole block on completion: no landing, no 'Worked for', and the reply jumps up | `apps/web/src/lib/components/tabs/views/ChatView.svelte:1990` | Keep the block mounted through the landing whenever it showed during the stream, by adding a `landingFor` flag the view holds for 2000ms. Then either keep a one-line 'Worked for 3s' for every turn, so the result is the same for every model, or collapse the row height over --motion-layout (300ms) on --ease-premium after the landing ends. Do not let it disappear in a single frame. | partly: Core confirmed. ChatView.svelte:1990 mounts the block only for hasThinkingContent or while streaming. hasThinkingContent (transcript.ts:146) is false for a plain reply, so the block unmounts when turnPhase.working drops. The landing (ThinkingBlock.svelte:206-225) and 'Worked for' (322-328) never render. Reasoning parts count toward hasThinkingContent, so whether 'Worked for' stays depends on the model. The 27.5px header plus 10px margin (426-447) is right. The 'reply jumps up' detail is overstated. ChatView.svelte:1419-1427 calls holdReadingPosition at the end of every turn, and on each rAF it pins the block found at the reading line. When that anchor is in or below the reply, the collapse is corrected before paint. The shift shows only when the anchor sits above the block, for example the question at the top of a short view. |
| working-2 | high | bug | Code-interpreter charts are drawn twice in the same reply | `apps/web/src/lib/components/tabs/views/ChatView.svelte:2014` | Draw each figure once, at the place in the reply where the call happened. Write alt text from the model's caption or the call's title rather than the file name. | confirmed |
| working-3 | high | motion | Movement changes are visible: opacity snaps and the 4-dot precess pops at every loop | `apps/web/src/lib/components/ThinkingMark.svelte:324` | Give every script p=0 and p=1 poses that both equal REST: scale syllogism's pulse so its loop boundary sits at opacity 1 and r 3, or have it pulse down from full ink. Ramp precess in and out: tilt 0→56° over p 0-0.12 and 56°→0 over 0.88-1, with m3 following the same envelope. Alternatively, crossfade between the old and new pose over --motion-enter (180ms) whenever the movement changes. Set the markup's initial opacity to the first painted frame. Reorder the circles by z each frame (insertBefore), or keep a sorted index. | partly: (1) Confirmed by computation. At the end of a syllogism loop the dot opacities are about 0.34, 0.35 and 0.41 (PULSE_OPACITY/APEX_OPACITY at 133-136, STAGGER 138). Every 3D script at p=0 has k=1, so opacity is about 1.0 (242). The switch at 324-330 jumps between them, in both directions. (2) Confirmed. precess holds tilt 56 throughout (113) and m3 ends at 1 (116), while fourth ends flat (103,106), so switching snaps the tilt and each precess wrap retracts m3 from 1 to 0. The z-order point is also confirmed: paint() (269-278) ignores Dot[4], and ThreeBody.svelte:177 sorts by depth. (3) is doubtful. The mount $effect requests the rAF, which normally runs before the first paint, so the markup's r=3 and opacity 1 (381-383) are usually never painted. That part is inferred to be invisible in the common case. |

Feed it the in-progress parts when the assistant message appears instead of mounting a second one. | partly: Confirmed: `let running = "syllogism"` (ThinkingMark.svelte:296), and a change is taken only at the loop boundary (324-330), so a Deep Research turn shows 3 dots for up to 1.6s. The mounting rule is at design-grammar.md:344. Wrong detail: the pre-reply block gets toolCalls=[] with no reasoning or narration, so hasContent is false and it can never be expanded. No expanded state is lost. The double 'Working on a reply' announcement is plausible but inferred. |

### Composer

ChatView.changeMode (1090-1092) applies it with no confirmation, and nothing in the composer has aria-live. Wrong detail: the red border is not the only cue. ### Type, spacing, color and one stylesheet (chat replies, composer, pages, articles)
